# Learnings from the proof-architecture experimental thread

> Companion to [PROOF_ARCHITECTURES_PLAN.md](PROOF_ARCHITECTURES_PLAN.md) (test plan and status table) and the per-architecture READMEs. This document synthesizes what the experiments collectively **teach** about the four candidate proof architectures and the RH landscape, organized by finding rather than by architecture. Updated as new experiments complete.

The companion documents answer "what did each experiment do?". This one answers "what do they collectively tell us?". When a new experiment lands, look here for the cross-cutting interpretation.

---

## Cross-cutting findings

### 109. Lever B M-1 route B: O2 ("deg is a quadratic form") is now a THEOREM, not an assumption -- the Weil-pairing/symplectic-determinant mechanism formalized in Lean. The crux of the elementary-Hasse argument is O2: that the degree map is a quadratic form (equivalently, the dual isogeny is additive). Phase A (#107(e)) made it FREE in the abstract by observing det of a 2×2 IS a quadratic form, but left WHY deg=det as geometric input. This finding formalizes that why -- the Weil-pairing mechanism -- as pure linear algebra (new module lean/ZetaRH/TateModule.lean, build green Lean v4.30.0, all three #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]). Route B's structure: for ℓ≠char, T_ℓ E ≅ ℤ_ℓ² is free rank 2, End(E) acts, and the Weil pairing e_ℓ is alternating with e_ℓ(φx,φy)=e_ℓ(x,y)^{deg φ} (Silverman III.8.6), so an isogeny scales the pairing by its degree. Three theorems. (1) `weilForm x y = x₀y₁−x₁y₀` (the symplectic model of e_ℓ on ℤ²) + `weilForm_mulVec`: the SYMPLECTIC DETERMINANT LAW, weilForm(A·x)(A·y) = (det A)·weilForm(x,y) for any A : Matrix (Fin 2)(Fin 2) R over any CommRing R (proved by Matrix.det_fin_two + Fin.sum_univ_two + ring). THIS is the rank-2 reason deg=det: an endomorphism scales an alternating form by exactly its determinant. (2) `scaling_eq_det`: if A scales weilForm by a constant c then c=det A (evaluate at the standard basis where weilForm e₀ e₁=1). So once deg φ is the Weil-pairing exponent (the geometric input), it is FORCED to equal det of the rank-2 representation -- deg=det with no further work. (3) `functionfield_RH_of_weil_pairing`: the route-B endpoint stated INTRINSICALLY, with NO det in its hypotheses -- given the Frobenius matrix A, a degree function degOf m n presented as the factor by which m·1+n·φ scales the Weil pairing (hscale), and non-negative degrees (hnn), every Frobenius eigenvalue α has |α|²=det A over EVERY finite field (proof: scaling_eq_det turns hscale into degOf=det, hnn gives det(m·1+n·A)≥0, then functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general/#108 closes it). NET: route B's O2 is now machine-checked (it IS the symplectic determinant law), so the elementary-Hasse residual narrows from "O1+O2" to O1 ALONE: construct the rank-2 representation A = Frobenius on T_ℓ, the Weil pairing, and deg≥0 -- the scheme-theoretic content Mathlib still lacks (E[n]≅(ℤ/n)², the Weil pairing, T_ℓ; all FLT targets). This also makes Route B the clear recommendation over Route A′: O2 is not just "free" but already FORMALIZED here, and Route B's deps are FLT-aligned. The deg=det/symplectic-determinant pattern is the literal finite-field rehearsal of the M4 target (deg=det=norm is a quadratic form whose positivity is RH). Cross-refs: #108 (the matrix endpoint this feeds, functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general), #107 (Phase A: det-of-2×2-is-quadratic, the abstract O2 this grounds geometrically), #FF-M1B (VERIFIER target), 08A M4 (the Spec(Z) generalization). Plan: docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md (task 3.5 DONE; O1 the sole residual). NON-VACUITY CHECK (same session, IsogenyDegree.lean, axiom-clean): to guard against the matrix endpoints being TRUE-BUT-VACUOUS (the project's #106 failure mode, the false-vacuous hodge_index_curve_elliptic sorry), the explicit witness is exhibited -- `companion t q = !![0,-q;1,t]` (the companion matrix of X²−tX+q, trace t, det q) with `companion_degForm` (det(m·1+n·companion)=m²+tmn+qn², via det_smul_one_add_smul) and `companion_degForm_nonneg` (for any Hasse-valid (q,t), i.e. t²≤4q, the companion's isogeny degrees are ≥0, via 4·degForm=(2m+tn)²+(4q−t²)n²≥0). So the matrix-endpoint hypothesis is SATISFIABLE and exactly equivalent to Hasse (no stronger assumption smuggled in), and the degForm endpoint (#107) and matrix endpoint (#108) coincide through the companion witness. This is a CONSISTENCY check, NOT a step in the RH proof: it assumes Hasse to produce deg≥0, the reverse of the real chain (where deg≥0 is geometric and Hasse is its consequence).

### 108. Lever B M-1 Phase A endpoint WIRED (task 5): the route-B chain from "Frobenius is the integer matrix A, deg=det" to "RH for the curve" is now machine-checked end-to-end, sorry-free and axiom-clean; the SOLE open input is the existence of A. The Phase A bridge `hasse_of_matrix` (#107(e)) showed a rank-2 integer matrix A with non-negative isogeny degrees (det(m·1+n·A) ≥ 0 on the lattice) has (tr A)² ≤ 4·det A, but it did not connect the matrix to the eigenvalue extraction (#FF-1). This finding closes that loop in lean/ZetaRH/FunctionFieldRH.lean (build green, 3638 jobs, Lean v4.30.0; all three new #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] only). Two theorems. (1) `matrix_charpoly_root`: a complex α in the spectrum of the complexified Frobenius matrix `A.map (Int.castRingHom ℂ)` is a root of X² − (tr A)·X + (det A). This GROUNDS the previously-abstract Frobenius "roots" of the chain (the bare hypothesis `α² − tα + q = 0`) in the genuine eigenvalues of the rank-2 representation, via Mathlib's `Matrix.mem_spectrum_iff_isRoot_charpoly` (over the field ℂ, spectrum = char-poly roots) + `Matrix.charpoly_fin_two` (charpoly of a 2×2 = X² − C(trace)·X + C(det)), with trace/det commuting through the cast (`AddMonoidHom.map_trace`, `RingHom.map_det` via `RingHom.mapMatrix_apply`). (2) `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix` (the route-B headline): over a PRIME field, a rank-2 integer Frobenius matrix A with det A = p and non-negative isogeny degrees forces every Frobenius eigenvalue α (the complex spectrum of A) to have |α|² = p — the function-field Riemann Hypothesis for the curve. Proof = hasse_of_matrix (Hasse bound) → hasse_strict_of_prime (strict over a prime field, 4p not a perfect square) → root_nonreal + eigenvalue_modulus (#FF-1, Vieta on the conjugate). An intermediate `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_root` exposes the abstract-root form for composition. NET: this is task 5 ("Wire") of the lever-B plan and the literal finite-field rehearsal of the M4 target ("deg = det = norm is a quadratic form whose positivity is RH", here proved at genus 1 over a prime field). Everything downstream of the matrix A — the deg=det quadratic-form structure, the Hasse bridge, the strict boundary, the eigenvalue grounding, the eigenvalue extraction — is now sorry-free and axiom-clean. The ONLY remaining open input across the entire chain is the EXISTENCE of A: the scheme-theoretic Frobenius-on-Tate-module construction (O1+O2, route B: E[n]≅(ℤ/n)² → Weil pairing → T_ℓ E ≅ ℤ² → deg=det on T_ℓ; or route A′: dual isogeny + theorem of the cube), the genuine Mathlib-absent / FLT-adjacent contribution. Plumbing note: `RingHom.map_det f M` is stated in the `f.mapMatrix M` form, NOT `M.map f`, so rewrite needs `← RingHom.mapMatrix_apply` first; `AddMonoidHom.map_trace` is already in the `A.map f` form. Cross-refs: #107 (M-1 algebraic core + Phase A bridge this completes), #FF-1 (the eigenvalue extraction this grounds + invokes), #106 (the conditional chain), #105/#104 (the AHK P3 / coupling faces of the same deg-carries-t geometry), 08A M4 (the Spec(Z) Rosati generalization this rehearses). Plan: docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md (task 5 DONE; O1+O2 the sole residual). VERIFIER: #FF-M1 row updated in lean/README.md. BOUNDARY CASE DONE (same session, all finite fields not just prime): `eigenvalue_modulus_le` extracts |α|²=q from only the NON-strict Hasse bound t²≤4q (vs the strict t²<4q the original eigenvalue_modulus/root_nonreal needed), by casing on whether α is real -- non-real ⟹ eigenvalue_modulus (Vieta on the conjugate, needs only non-realness); real ⟹ the root equation α.re²−t·α.re+q=0 plus t²≤4q force (α.re−t/2)²=(t²−4q)/4 ≤ 0, so (sq_eq_zero_iff) α.re=t/2 and |α|²=α.re²=(t/2)²=q. This handles the supersingular boundary t²=4q (real double eigenvalue ±√q) that the strict chain could not. `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix_general` then drops the prime-field restriction of functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_matrix: for ANY rank-2 integer Frobenius matrix A with non-negative isogeny degrees (det(m·1+n·A)≥0), every eigenvalue has |α|²=det A, over every finite field 𝔽_q (q=det A=deg Frobenius), prime or prime-power. Both sorry-free, axiom-clean. So the M-4 boundary wire (flagged in the plan) is closed; the prime-field endpoints remain as sharper-statement specializations. Plumbing: push_cast has a norm_cast lemma that pushes ↑(M.det) INTO (M.map ↑).det (toward the leaves), which mangles a `((A.det:ℝ):ℂ)` goal -- convert such ofReal∘intCast casts directly with `Complex.ofReal_intCast` instead of push_cast when a det/trace term is present.

### 107. Lever B milestone M-1 (the isogeny DEGREE API, algebraic core) is DONE sorry-free: the Hasse bound is now DERIVED from degree-positivity, not assumed. The lever-B chain previously carried the Hasse bound as a raw numeric field `hodge_index : t^2 < 4q` of `EllipticFrobeniusData` (#106). M-1 pushes that assumption back to its true geometric source. New module lean/ZetaRH/IsogenyDegree.lean (#FF-M1, build green Lean v4.30.0, #print axioms = [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] only) defines the Frobenius degree form `degForm t q m n = m^2 + t*m*n + q*n^2` (= deg(m.1 + n.phi), with deg 1 = 1, deg phi = q, cross term = the trace t = q+1-#E) and proves the **Hasse bridge** `disc_nonpos_of_int_nonneg`: if the degree form is non-negative on the endomorphism lattice Z.1 (+) Z.phi -- the genuine geometric content "every isogeny has non-negative degree" -- then t^2 <= 4q. The proof has two clean steps: (i) integer -> rational by homogeneity (degForm scales as 1/k^2 under (m,n) -> (m/k,n/k), so non-negativity on Z gives it on Q), then (ii) rational -> real by density (`disc_nonpos_of_rat_nonneg`: if g(x)=x^2+tx+q >= 0 on all rationals then t^2<=4q, via completing the square g(x)=(x+t/2)^2 - (t^2/4-q) and `exists_rat_btwn` to pin a rational within sqrt(eps) of -t/2). This ABSORBS the milestone M-3 integer->real "density glue" that M-0 had punted. Strict prime boundary `hasse_strict_of_prime` handles the milestone M-4 prime-field boundary: t^2<=4p upgrades to the strict t^2<4p because `four_mul_prime_not_isSquare` (if a^2=4p then a=2b and b^2=p, so b | p forces b=1 or b=p, both => p=1, absurd) rules out the equality case. Wired in FunctionFieldRH.lean by the constructor `EllipticFrobeniusData.ofDegreeNonneg {p prime, tz : Z} (hdeg : deg >= 0 on the lattice)` and the endpoint `functionfield_RH_elliptic_of_degree`: over a prime field, deg >= 0 on the lattice => |alpha|^2 = p (the curve's zeta zeros on Re=1/2), entirely sorry-free. NET: the Hasse bound is now a THEOREM downstream of degree-positivity, and milestones M-3 (density) + M-4 (prime boundary) fall out of the same construction. The whole elementary-Hasse argument is machine-checked EXCEPT the one irreducible scheme-theoretic fact -- that deg actually IS this non-negative quadratic form for a real curve (isogenies, additivity of deg, deg >= 0) -- which Mathlib lacks and which is now isolated as the single hypothesis `hdeg` (the residual M-1, the genuine FLT-project-adjacent contribution). This is the correct division of labour: the positive-definite-quadratic-form -> Hasse bound logic (the same pattern as 08A's genus-g Rosati M1 and the Spec(Z) target M4) is now a reusable, axiom-clean Lean lemma; only the curve-to-form geometry remains. Cross-refs: #106 (#FF-geom carried as hypothesis, the chain this completes), #FF-M0 (the real-form discriminant bridge this generalizes to the lattice), #2G-1 (the keystone, the negative mirror on the intersection form), #105/#104 (the AHK P3 / coupling faces of the same degree-map-carries-t geometry), 08A M1/M4 (the Rosati generalization). Plan + status: docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md (M-1 algebraic core DONE, scheme-theoretic layer residual). FOLLOW-ON (M-1.5 + discharge strategy, 2026-06-14): (a) M-1.5 built sorry-free in the same file (axiom-clean) -- `quadratic_eq_basis` (a Mathlib `QuadraticForm ℤ (ℤ×ℤ)` is FORCED to `m²·Q(1,0)+t·mn+n²·Q(0,1)` via `map_smul`+`polar_smul_left/right`) and `hasse_of_quadratic` (a non-negative quadratic form with `Q(1,0)=1` has `t²≤4q`), which reduces the residual hypothesis `hdeg` from "the explicit form is nonneg" to the honest minimum "deg is a non-negative quadratic form with two pinned values". (b) A re-audit of the pinned Mathlib (v4.30.0) for how to discharge that residual found MORE present than assumed: `WeierstrassCurve.localPolynomial = 1-aT+qT²` (LFunction.lean, T. Browning 2026) already DEFINES the Frobenius trace `a=q+1-#E` and the point count, so obligation O3 is largely in Mathlib; division polynomials give `deg[n]=n²` (Φ_n degree n²); reduction mod p is present; ABSENT (grep, zero hits) are isogenies/End/deg, the dual isogeny + additivity, the Weil pairing, the Tate module (and FLT is not a dependency). (c) The residual decomposes into four obligations -- O1 (End+deg), O2 (deg is a quadratic form = additivity of the dual, the CRUX), O3 (two values+trace, mostly in Mathlib), O4 (deg≥0) -- and "deg is a quadratic form ⟺ the dual isogeny is additive" (Silverman III.6). Three routes: A (elementary, partial), A′ (dual isogeny, theorem-of-the-cube, minimal self-contained surface), B (deg=det on the Tate module, makes O2 FREE since det of a 2×2 is a quadratic form, FLT-aligned, and is the literal finite-field rehearsal of the M4 "deg=det/norm on H¹" shape). Recommendation: B for the program, A′ for the wedge. Upstreamable endpoint: the Hasse bound for `WeierstrassCurve.localPolynomial`. Full strategy + dependency-ordered task list in the plan doc. (d) O3 WIRING DONE (lean/ZetaRH/LocalFactor.lean, #FF-O3, axiom-clean): the chain is now connected to Mathlib's actual local L-factor. `localFactor_root_normSq` (RH for the abstract local factor `1-aT+qT²`: `a²<4q` ⟹ every complex root has `|·|²=1/q` -- proved via the reciprocal `α=β⁻¹`, since the local factor `(1-αT)(1-βT)` has roots = reciprocals of the Frobenius eigenvalues, then reuses #FF-1's eigenvalue_modulus/root_nonreal); `localPolynomial_eq_of_goodReduction` (unfolds `WeierstrassCurve.localPolynomial` in the good-reduction case to `1 - C a·X + C q·X²` with `a=q+1-#W(κ)`, `q=#κ`); `localPolynomial_root_normSq` (the roots of Mathlib's `localPolynomial` have `|·|²=1/q`, given the Hasse bound). So the trace/count input is now Mathlib-native (taken from T. Browning's `localPolynomial` def, not abstract), and the ONLY open input across the whole lever-B chain is the Hasse bound `a²<4q` = the `hdeg`/#FF-geom residual = the O1+O2 scheme-theoretic degree-map work. (e) PHASE A DONE (IsogenyDegree.lean, #FF-M1, axiom-clean): the deg=det route-agnostic bridge. `det_smul_one_add_smul` (the 2×2 mixed-determinant identity `det(m·1+n·A) = m² + tr(A)·m·n + det(A)·n²`, proved via Matrix.det_fin_two/trace_fin_two) + `hasse_of_matrix` (for `A : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ`, `det(m·1+n·A) ≥ 0` for all integer m,n ⟹ `(tr A)² ≤ 4·det A`, by feeding the identity to disc_nonpos_of_int_nonneg). This makes "deg is a quadratic form" FREE in the Tate-module route: det of a 2×2 is automatically a quadratic form (it IS degForm with t=tr A, q=det A), so route B's O2 needs no theorem of the cube. SHARPENS the M-1 residual from "deg is a non-negative quadratic form" to the concrete "construct the rank-2 integer Frobenius representation A with det(m·1+n·A) ≥ 0 (every isogeny has nonneg degree, deg=det), det A = q, trace A = a = q+1-#E". A two-Explore-agent Mathlib audit (this session) confirmed both routes need ~40% build: route B (E[n] → E[n]≅(ℤ/n)² → Weil pairing → T_ℓ → deg=det, all absent; linear-algebra core present: LinearMap.det/charpoly=X²-tr·X+det/QuadraticForm), route A′ (isogeny/End/deg/dual/Pic⁰/theorem-of-the-cube absent; reusable: Point AddCommGroup, ClassGroup + Point.toClass, division polys deg Φₙ=n², Scheme.Hom.finrank). Full M-1 plan (Phase A done + Phase B routes, dependency chains): the lever_b plan doc + the saved plan file. Lean lemma plumbing note: `aeval β (C n)` for `n : ℤ` needs `eq_intCast` (C is a ℤ-ring-hom, so `C n` normalizes to `↑n`) + `map_intCast` (aeval sends intCast to intCast), NOT `aeval_C` (which leaves a residual `algebraMap`); and generic `map_add`/`map_sub` distribute over the COEFFICIENT's `+`/`-` unless the coefficient is `set` opaque first.

### 106. Lever B's function-field RH chain is now FULLY SORRY-FREE (a conditional theorem), and a latent false-sorry was fixed. Discharging #FF-geom (lean/ZetaRH/FunctionFieldRH.lean) revealed that the geometric step `hodge_index_curve_elliptic` was a sorry standing in for a FALSE proposition: it claimed `NegDef 1 q t` for ALL q,t behind a vacuous `True` hypothesis (false whenever t^2 >= 4q, e.g. t=3,q=1), since NegDef 1 q t <=> t^2 < 4q by the keystone. The honest repair: the Castelnuovo-Severi / Hodge-index inequality t^2 < 4q is the genuine geometric content, so it is now carried as an explicit hypothesis FIELD (`hodge_index`) of a new `EllipticFrobeniusData` structure, from which `negDef_of_curve` derives NegDef sorry-free (via the keystone #2G-1) and `functionfield_RH_elliptic` derives |alpha|=sqrt q sorry-free. The whole file is now SORRY-FREE (build green, 3147 jobs, Lean v4.30.0; grep confirms no sorry in code, only comment mentions), i.e. a sorry-free CONDITIONAL theorem: GIVEN Castelnuovo-Severi for a genus-1 curve, the Frobenius eigenvalues have |alpha|=sqrt q. The single unformalized input (the geometric inequality) is now an explicit, TRUE hypothesis rather than an admitted (and here false-as-stated) sorry. Discharging it -- proving Castelnuovo-Severi from a curve's definition -- still needs Mathlib algebraic-curve intersection theory (absent), so the gap is unchanged in substance but the formalization is now honest and composable. This is the correct end-state for lever B's function-field scaffold: the eigenvalue-extraction end (#FF-1) + the assembly are machine-checked, the geometric input (#FF-geom) is an explicit hypothesis, and the latent unsoundness is removed. VERIFIER targets #FF-1 / #FF-geom updated (both no longer sorry). Cross-refs: #FF-1/#105/#104 (the AHK/coupling face of the same geometry), #2G-1 (the keystone the chain wires), the lever-B scoping in optimizing_rh_for_ai.md. FOLLOW-ON: a formalization plan to make the chain UNCONDITIONAL (discharge #FF-geom via the elementary-Hasse / degree-quadratic-form route, NOT surface intersection theory; Mathlib has the group law but not isogeny/degree/Hasse; milestones M-0..M-4) is docs/03_research/lever_b_function_field_plan.md. Milestone M-0 DONE (#FF-M0): the discriminant bridge lemma proved sorry-free (disc_nonpos_of_posSemidef: a positive-semidefinite binary form m^2+t.mn+q.n^2 has t^2 <= 4q; disc_neg_of_posDef: positive-definite gives strict t^2 < 4q), the positive mirror of #2G-1 and the bridge from "deg >= 0 for isogenies" to the Hasse bound; the remaining glue (integer->real by homogeneity+density) is M-3, the isogeny+degree API (M-1) is the critical path (~months, coordinate with the FLT project).

### 105. The AHK arithmetic lattice (09A), instantiated on the smallest case (9A.1-9A.3), NARROWS the BUILDER target from six properties to ONE: the gap is P3 (the t-carrying degree map); P1/P2/P4/P5 are already combinatorially free. [e2uu_ahk_lattice_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2uu_ahk_lattice_attempt.py) ran the first instantiation of the 09A spec. (1) 9A.3 (FF specialization) VERIFIED: on NS(C x C) genus 1, the degree map gives Gamma.Delta = #C(F_q) = q+1-t (P3 holds, carries t) and the primitive form is negative-definite iff |t|<2 sqrt q (P6 = the Weil bound), matched for (q,t) in {(5,1),(5,3),(7,2),(13,4)} -- the spec is right where M4 is a theorem. (2) 9A.1-9A.2 (abstract lattice) done on the Boolean lattice B_2 on {2,3}: P1 holds (the rank-gen polynomial (1+x)^2 factors over the atoms = the product-star structure), P2 holds (Whitney numbers (1,2,1) rank-symmetric), P4 + P5 hold by AHK 2018 + #48/e3r -- BUT P5 is UNCONDITIONAL (t-blind: the same (1,n-1) for every weighting), and the degree map is a combinatorial integer (# maximal chains = 2) with NO t-slot, so P3 FAILS. (3) VERDICT: the bare combinatorial lattice already supplies P1/P2/P4/P5; the SINGLE missing property is P3 (the t-carrying degree map = q+1-t on the FF shadow), and P3 is exactly what makes P6 (the primitive polarization) t-dependent and RH-meaningful. SPEC REFINEMENT fed back to 09A: P5 is DEMOTED (the (1,n-1) is free + t-blind, #48, not the discriminator; the earlier "primitive form is indefinite (1,n-1)" wording is corrected -- the (1,n-1) is the FULL form, the PRIMITIVE part is negative-definite and IS the polarization, the #101/e2qq carries-t-not-definite-vs-indefinite refinement); P3 is ELEVATED as THE gap. So the AHK BUILDER target NARROWS from "build a 6-property object" to "build a graded prime-lattice whose degree map yields q+1-t"; everything else is combinatorial, and P3 (over Z: an arithmetic intersection theory whose Lefschetz numbers are the local Frobenius traces) is the AHK face of the e2tt coupling (#104) = M4. The genuine division of labour: the combinatorics is free; the arithmetic decoration (P3 => P6) is everything. Not a solve (P3 over Z is the t-carrying arithmetic structure = the missing cohomology), but a sharp narrowing of the target + a clean refinement of 09A. Cross-refs: #104 (the coupling, this is the AHK face), #48/e3r (convex-Hodge (1,n-1) unconditional = P5 free), #40 (t-blindness), #101/e2qq (carries-t refinement), #97 (the sharpened AHK target this executes), 2G (the FF NS(C x C) the spec specializes to). [e2uu .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2uu_ahk_lattice_attempt.md); 09A section 6A.

### 104. The MC.4 coupling cannot be SOURCED from the modular carrier (AT-1 closed in the negative), and the coupling has FOUR unified faces, all = M4. [e2tt_coupling_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2tt_coupling_attempt.py) executes the e2ss/#103 adversary test AT-1: is there a coupling between the per-prime twisted blocks sourced ONLY from the modular/KMS data (beta>1) that forces |t_p|<2 sqrt p? Answer NO, three assertions: (1) the carrier data is t-INDEPENDENT (the BC modular weight spectrum {log n - log m} (81 weights), the Gibbs weights {n^{-beta}}, and any carrier-derived coupling e.g. (pq)^{-beta} I are functions of (prime set, beta) ALONE; the carrier never takes a trace as input, t enters only via C_E, MC.2/#101); (2) the constraint |t_p|<2 sqrt p lives in C_E (t-dependent), not the carrier (a t-independent operator cannot encode a t-dependent constraint), so no carrier-sourced coupling forces the bound; (3) FF template: the cup block B_E = -G_prim is positive-definite (the Hodge index, a theorem) exactly on |t|<2 sqrt q and CARRIES t (t=0,2,4 in-window PD, t=5 off-window not) = the target shape, a t-carrying GLOBAL cup, not a t-independent carrier coupling. NET: the coupling is IRREDUCIBLE to the carrier (which supplies only the t-independent scaffolding, the weight ladder + FE duality MC.1); it must be a t-carrying coupling of the C_E polarization phases = the global cup product. THE SYNTHESIS (the genuinely useful consolidation): the coupling is ONE object with FOUR presentations, all open over Spec(Z) and all = M4 -- (i) modular: a t-carrying coupling of the C_E phases (this thread); (ii) function-field: the cup H^1 x H^1 -> H^2, neg-def primitive = Castelnuovo-Severi (2G, a THEOREM); (iii) AHK: the t-carrying submodular Lefschetz + indefinite primitive form on the arithmetic prime-lattice (09A); (iv) Arakelov: the Faltings-Hriljac product + the Gamma_S place (2H is the single-surface theorem). The modular / AHK / product-surface threads are NOT separate attacks; they are four windows onto the same missing coupling. HONEST SCOPE: a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate (rules out the "clever KMS coupling" hope) + a unification; it does NOT solve M4 and nothing cheap can (the coupling must carry t; the only available t-carrying data is the zeros (circular) or a new global object). Next on this front is construction-grade: build one of the four faces (09A the most explicitly scoped) or formalize the FF cup in Lean (#FF-geom). Cross-refs: #103 (MC.4, the residual), #101 (C_E carries t, log Delta does not), #100/#102 (scaffolding + euler-gating), #97 (sourced-not-propagated), #70 (C_E), #42 (continuation/global-assembly gap), #68 (transport), #2G/09A/2H (the other three faces). [e2tt .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2tt_coupling_attempt.md).

### 103. MC.4 (= M4) on the smallest case is K1-CIRCULAR, with a sharp new mechanism: the modular/KMS carrier lives at Re(s)>1, so it is BLIND to the off-line obstruction (which lives in 1/2<Re(s)<1); the residual is the per-block COUPLING (= the product-surface assembly), M4 undiminished. An adversarially-honest BUILDER attempt ([e2ss_mc4_attempt.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ss_mc4_attempt.py)) assembled the smallest-case modular carrier: the BC truncation on a finite prime set P, with the genus-1 Euler-Sen primitive block per prime, Q_p = Omega(.,C_E.) = B_E(p,t_p)/sqrt(4p-t_p^2) (PD iff |t_p|<2 sqrt p), assembled as the KMS-weighted block-direct-sum M = (+)_p p^{-beta} Q_p; "positive on the primitive part" = every |t_p|<2 sqrt p = local RH at every prime. The disciplines run (56-row sweeps, e2ss_mc4_attempt.npz): fq-shadow PASSES (twist PD exactly on the Hasse-Weil window, 0 mismatches), K2 PASSES by construction (zeta's comb >=0 so the carrier forms; D-H's comb negative at n=3 so it does not, inherited from MC.3/#102), but K1 FAILS (circular): the modular weight grading log Delta is t-INDEPENDENT (529 weights, identical for every trace assignment), so the carrier does NOT supply t_p; positivity = (every |t_p|<2 sqrt p) = the conclusion. THE SHARP MECHANISM (the genuinely new content): the BC/KMS carrier lives at beta>1 i.e. Re(s)>1 (absolute convergence of the Euler product), while the local GL_2 factor 1 - t_p p^{-s} + p^{1-2s} has its zeros on Re(s)=1/2 at |t_p|=2 sqrt p, splits off the line for 2 sqrt p<|t_p|, and reaches Re(s)>1 (carrier-visible) only at the full Hasse bound |t_p|=p+1; the ENTIRE nontrivial off-line range 2 sqrt p<|t_p|<p+1 is INVISIBLE to the carrier. That is exactly why log Delta is t-blind: the marginal-positivity wall localized per prime, the same shape as the M2.6 stealth window. It is NOT (yet) the #80 continuous-spectrum wall directly (the finite block-sum is full rank); #80 is what the GLOBAL coupling would hit if one forced t intrinsically rather than asserting it (the block-sum has no literal stealth window only because it is decoupled, the same coin as "positive iff you assume each t in-window"). RESIDUAL (the one sentence MC.4 needs): construct the COUPLING between the per-prime twisted blocks (the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed-trace pairing = the product-surface / Poincare-duality assembly) that FORCES |t_p|<2 sqrt p from the modular flow itself rather than asserting it block by block; the decoupled carrier cannot (log Delta is t-blind), so t must come from the coupling, which is M4 undiminished. This matches the #97 doctrine exactly: the signature must be SOURCED (from the coupling), never PROPAGATED (asserted per block). NET: the cheap modular-carrier milestones MC.1-MC.3 are done; MC.4 is confirmed to BE M4 (K1-circular at the smallest case), with the residual named as the coupling and a sharp Re(s)>1-vs-critical-strip mechanism for why the modular carrier cannot reach it. The modular framing did not move the wall (as #98 P1-P5=NONE predicted); it sharpened the statement of where the wall is. Lean handoff (VT-1/2/3, AT-1) recorded in the e2ss .md. Cross-refs: #102/#101/#100 (MC.3/2/1), #99 (trace route closed), #98 (frame-audit), #97 (sourced-not-propagated), #70 (C_E K1 underdetermination), #80 (continuous-spectrum wall), #52/M2.6 (the stealth window). [e2ss .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ss_mc4_attempt.md).

### 102. MC.3: the finite-prime modular (Bost-Connes) structure is EULER-GATED, so the modular polarization carrier passes the K2 firewall by construction. [e2rr_modular_euler_gating.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2rr_modular_euler_gating.py) checks the K2 leg of the modular-carrier program. The finite-prime modular structure is the BC Gibbs/KMS structure: Hamiltonian with eigenvalues {log n}, partition function Z(beta) = Tr e^{-beta H} = sum_n n^{-beta} = zeta(beta) = prod_p (1-p^{-beta})^{-1}, pole at beta=1 (type III_1, #81); its "interaction" is the von Mangoldt comb Lambda_f(n) = a_n log n - sum_{d|n,d<n} a_{n/d} Lambda_f(d), and the Euler product = the comb being non-negative + supported on prime powers (#90/#37). Three assertions hold: (1) zeta's comb is non-negative (min Lambda = 0 over n<=60, 0 negatives, supported exactly on prime powers) so the positive Gibbs weights / KMS state form; (2) D-H's comb goes NEGATIVE (Lambda_DH(3) = -0.3121 = -kappa log 3 in closed form, first negative at n=3 the first prime = 3 mod 5, 21 negatives by n<=60), so NO positive Gibbs state -> the finite-prime modular structure does NOT form (reproduces #90 from the modular side); (3) zeta(beta) is the Euler product (prod_{p<5000}(1-p^-2)^-1 = zeta(2), residual ~2e-5, a truncation tail) with the pole at beta=1 (Z(1.02)=50.6), while D-H has NO Euler product but DOES have the functional equation -- so the archimedean Gamma-factor is SHARED and inert for K2; the discriminator is purely the finite-prime comb (#44: K2 lives on F not Theta_Sen). NET: the finite-prime modular structure is euler-gated; the carrier built on it passes K2 BY CONSTRUCTION (D-H cannot form it), exactly as the oracle's NoEulerProduct and the Lean no_dh_cupTarget do. This completes the cheap modular-carrier milestones: MC.1 (#100, the modular structure supplies the weight ladder + FE duality the trace lacks), MC.2 (#101, it factors the gap: C_E and N are extra data beyond log Delta), MC.3 (#102, the finite-prime modular structure is euler-gated). The open kernel MC.4 is unchanged = M4: prove the C_E-twisted form on the finite-prime modular carrier is positive, carrying t, without RH input. Cross-refs: #100/#101 (MC.1/MC.2), #90 (the passivity comb), #81 (BC type III_1), #44 (the finite/archimedean split), #37 (the prime-side discriminator). [e2rr .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2rr_modular_euler_gating.md); modular_polarization_carrier.md MC.3.

### 101. MC.2, the modular-Weil link, FACTORS the M4 gap rather than closing it: the modular scaffolding is t-independent, and the t-carrying polarization needs C_E (the phase, != J) and the monodromy N (!= log Delta), with positivity the open kernel. [e2qq_modular_weil_link.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2qq_modular_weil_link.py) tests the #100 dictionary against the actual polarization data (the Weil operator C_E #70/e2lo and the Sen flow Theta #41) on the finite genus-1 Euler-Sen model (Omega=[[0,1],[-1,0]], B_E(q,t)=[[2,t],[t,2q]]=-G_prim, A_E=Omega^{-1}B_E, A_E^2=(t^2-4q)I, C_E=A_E(-A_E^2)^{-1/2}). Three assertions hold. (1) Theta = -1/2 I + N is NON-semisimple (N != 0, N^2 = 0), hence NOT self-adjoint, hence NOT log Delta for any state (log Delta is self-adjoint): the modular Hamiltonian matches only the SEMISIMPLE part of Theta (the weight -1/2), and the monodromy N is INVISIBLE to it (= #69, the Petrov non-semisimplicity; the weight filtration is modular, the monodromy is extra). (2) The Weil operator C_E is a complex-linear COMPLEX STRUCTURE (C_E^2 = -I), NOT the antilinear modular conjugation J (J^2 = +1, e2pp): the naive "J = C_E" is a CATEGORY ERROR; C_E is the polarization phase, J the real-structure conjugation, so C_E is EXTRA data beyond J. (3) The C_E-twisted polarization B_E CARRIES t (B_E(t=1) != B_E(t=3), positive-definite iff t^2 < 4q; off-line t=5 gives 4q-t^2 = -5 < 0 so C_E is not real and there is no polarization), whereas log Delta and any GNS trace form are t-INDEPENDENT: t is injected by C_E, not by the modular Hamiltonian. NET: the modular structure (e2pp/#100) supplies the t-independent scaffolding (the weight grading + the FE duality J Delta J = Delta^{-1}); the t-carrying polarization needs the phase C_E (!= J) and the monodromy N (!= log Delta); M4 is proving the assembled C_E-twisted form positive without RH input. The gap is FACTORED (modular scaffolding free + C_E + N + positivity), not closed. REFINEMENT OF #99/e2oo: both the trace form and B_E are positive-DEFINITE, so the genuine discriminator is CARRIES-t (B_E depends on t, the trace does not), NOT definite-vs-indefinite; the indefinite object is the cup form Omega / G_prim that C_E polarizes. This sharpens e2oo's verdict without changing it (the trace route is wrong because it is t-blind and structureless, not merely definite). Cross-refs: #100 (the dictionary this tests), #99 (the trace route closed, the phrasing refined), #70 (C_E = the phase), #69 (the monodromy N the modular Hamiltonian misses), #41 (Theta), #68 (transport: the C_E-twist must source the indefinite B), #40 (t-blindness). [e2qq .md](arithmetic_geometric/e2qq_modular_weil_link.md); modular_polarization_carrier.md MC.2.

### 100. The modular-carrier PoC: the type III modular structure SUPPLIES the weight grading + the FE/Poincare duality the trace lacks (the positive counterpart of #99), unifying C_E / Theta / the weight ladder as one Tomita-Takesaki object. #99 closed the trace route and steered: the M4 polarization must ride on the MODULAR structure of the type III_1 arithmetic algebra (the modular flow = the Frobenius/scaling dynamics), not a trace. [e2pp_modular_weight.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2pp_modular_weight.py) tests the cheap feasibility question that steer implies (does the modular structure supply what the trace cannot?) on a finite Tomita-Takesaki model phi(x)=Tr(rho x) on M_n, three assertions all holding: (1) a TRACE is weightless (rho=I/n => Delta=I, log Delta spectrum all 0), so it cannot carry the Hodge weight filtration; (2) a MODULAR (non-tracial) state supplies a nontrivial weight grading (log Delta spectrum {log(rho_i/rho_j)} = the candidate weight ladder, #93 Poincare halving / Frobenius moduli q^(w/2)); (3) the modular conjugation J is an antilinear involution that DUALIZES Delta: J^2=1 and J Delta J = Delta^{-1} exactly (residual 4.4e-16), so the weight spectrum is symmetric under negation (Gr_w <-> Gr_-w = the s<->1-s functional equation). So the modular (type III) structure supplies, FOR FREE, the weight ladder AND the FE/Poincare duality the trace lacks; this is why "the arithmetic lives in the modular structure, not the trace". THE DICTIONARY (docs/03_research/modular_polarization_carrier.md): modular flow sigma_t = Frobenius/scaling flow (= Theta, #41); log Delta = the weight operator (= the weight filtration #93); J = the Weil/Hodge C-operator (= C_E, #70); J Delta J = Delta^{-1} = FE/Poincare duality (perfectness, free, #61); type III_1 = no trace (#81, #99); and the OPEN part = the J-twisted INDEFINITE form Q(.,J.) being a polarization that carries t (= M4, 08A). So the realization+duality half is the modular structure (free); the signature/positivity half is M4, unchanged. HONEST SCOPE: this is a SHAPE-SHARPENER, not a shortcut: the frame-audit (#98) already scored P1-P5 (Connes/Tomita-Takesaki KMS) = NONE, so the modular route does NOT make the blind spot cheap; the K1 risk is the Lagarias/Connes lossless-on-the-line positivity (R3.5-circular), the K2 discriminator must live in the FINITE/Frobenius half of Delta (D-H shares the archimedean Gamma half, #44). The value is unification (C_E #70 + the weight ladder #93 + the flow Theta #41 = one Tomita-Takesaki structure) + a precise BUILDER target (the polarization is the J-twisted indefinite form, not the tracial one; milestones MC.1-MC.4). Cross-refs: #99 (the steer + the trace route closed), #70 (C_E = J), #81 (BC type III_1), #41 (Theta = log Delta / the flow), #93 (the weight ladder = log Delta spectrum), #44 (F vs Theta_Sen = the finite/archimedean split for K2), #68 (the form is transport; the J-twist must source the indefinite B), 08A (the Rosati ladder, where MC.4 = M4 lives).

### 99. The P3-P4 von Neumann probe CLOSES the one live frame-audit edge: a faithful trace upgrades the edge only to the WRONG positivity (definite + D-H-blind), so P5 stays isolated and the residual is NAMED. The frame-audit (#98) left exactly one live edge, P3-P4 (noncircular <-> euler-gated), CORRELATED in the Rosati/Frobenius-algebra frame, with the sharpest next probe: does REQUIRING the arithmetic Frobenius algebra A to be a finite von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal tracial state upgrade CORRELATED -> GENUINE (make "A exists and is euler-gated" co-extensive with "A carries an intrinsic positive Rosati involution")? [e2oo_vonneumann_rosati_probe.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2oo_vonneumann_rosati_probe.py) answers it on small models, four structural assertions all holding: (1) a faithful trace DOES give an intrinsic positive form for free (the GNS Gram tau(b* a) on M_2 is positive-definite, min eig 0.5), so the upgrade's premise holds; (2) BUT that form is positive-DEFINITE by construction (tau(x x*) >= 0, = 0 iff x = 0), signature (4,0,0), so it can NEVER be M4's polarization, which is NOT positive-definite (the full Hodge index is indefinite (1,n-1); equivalently the 2G primitive form is negative-definite under Hasse-Weil) - the very faithfulness that makes the upgrade work is what fixes the WRONG signature; (3) the natural arithmetic state is a KMS state with a nontrivial modular flow (the scaling/Frobenius dynamics), NOT a trace (omega(E12 E21)=0.731 != 0.269=omega(E21 E12) at beta=1), i.e. Bost-Connes is type III_1 (#81) with NO faithful normal trace, so the hypothesis is not even satisfied by the natural algebra; (4) the trace form is D-H-BLIND (defined for any *-algebra, needs no Euler product; exists for zeta AND D-H), a soft positivity, not the euler-gated M4 form. NET: the upgrade reaches P3-from-P4 only as a DEFINITE, D-H-blind positivity on a type II_1 algebra the arithmetic does not provide (it is type III_1). So the edge is CORRELATED, not GENUINE, for the RH-relevant (indefinite, euler-gated) positivity; P5 stays isolated (confirms #98's prediction). The named residual: M4 = (indefinite (1,n-1), euler-gated Hodge form) minus (definite trace form) = the 2MM transport result (#68) in operator-algebra language (a faithful trace fixes the form PD; M4 needs it indefinite). A sharp NEGATIVE coordinate: it closes the "make P3-P4 an iff via a trace" route and tells the construction work that the polarization on A must be the indefinite MODULAR/Hodge form (the type III modular flow sigma_t = the Frobenius/scaling dynamics is where the arithmetic lives), not the tracial one. Cross-refs: #98 (the frame-audit + the value-signal program), #81 (BC type III_1, the KMS machinery reused), #68 (2MM transport), #43 (de Branges, another wrong-positivity), 08A (the Rosati ladder). ### 98. The regime-two FRAME-AUDIT is the FIFTH all-roads convergence, and it yields a new structural datum: the M4 property graph is two cheap edges around the Euler-product vertex with the blind spot ISOLATED. The one unspent discovery axis (#97) was "does a distant frame collapse two of the M4 conjuncts into ONE statement, as the acoustic frame collapsed carries-trace into passivity?". Run as a structured 11-agent workflow over all ten pairs of the five M4 properties (P1 carries-trace, P2 global, P3 noncircular, P4 euler-gated, P5 = the blind-spot indefinite (1,n-1) polarization), each pair searched for a co-extensive (iff) frame, each genuine claim adversarially verified. RESULT: nine pairs NONE, one (P3-P4) CORRELATED, ZERO new genuine collapses survived = the fifth independent convergence (after #90-94 acoustic, #95 Lee-Yang, #96 the 7-candidate screen, #97 the proof-engine sweep). TWO sharp sub-results. (a) P5 IS AN ISOLATED VERTEX: every pair containing the blind spot (P1/P2/P3/P4-P5) returned NONE in the strongest available frames (Connes/Tomita-Takesaki KMS with de Branges as its analytic face; Bombieri-Weil explicit-formula positivity on the adele class space; Hodge-Riemann/AHK; Bost-Connes KMS), so NO cheap property is co-extensive with P5; the AHK Hodge-Riemann frame, the only R3.5-escaping frame even capable of touching P5, was examined and found NOT to deliver the iff unconditionally = the marginal-positivity wall restated as an audit (the one frame that could jackpot is exactly the one whose verdict is "not unconditional"). (b) THE NEW DATUM: the property graph over {P1..P5} has exactly TWO edges, both at the cheap/cheap layer and both sharing the Euler-product vertex P4: P1-P4 (acoustic, known, #90-94) and P3-P4 (Rosati/Frobenius-algebra, CORRELATED). The "easy four" are NOT five independent asks; they are coalescing around P4 while P5 stays a lone island. Marginal positivity re-derived from graph connectivity. The D-H discipline held throughout (every NONE on a P4-pair is the firewall: no Euler product => no object). HIGHEST-VALUE NEXT PROBE surfaced: attack the live edge, not the dead island. Can P3-P4 be UPGRADED from CORRELATED to GENUINE by requiring the arithmetic Frobenius algebra A to be a finite von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal positive trace state (not a bare *-algebra)? I.e. does "A exists and is euler-gated" become co-extensive with "A carries an intrinsic positive Rosati involution"? If yes it names the residual to P5 as one concrete operator-algebra deficiency; cheap to falsify against the existing BC/KMS machinery + the D-H control (does the BC trace state induce a Rosati dagger whose positivity = euler-gating?). Expected NONE-or-CORRELATED, but it interrogates NEW structure.

This finding closes the "optimize RH for an AI to attack" session ([optimizing_rh_for_ai.md](../docs/03_research/optimizing_rh_for_ai.md)), whose thesis is that the binding constraint is the value-signal blind spot (no gradient at M4), so the optimization is to MANUFACTURE gradients + SHRINK the gradient-free kernel. Two apparatus deliverables shipped alongside the frame-audit. **Lever A (built):** the proven-case shadow BATTERY ([shadow_battery.py](lemma_db/shadow_battery.py) + test 6/6) generalizes the function-field shadow (6d) from one mirror into a graded battery of five proven cases (CP-fq Weil, CP-hodge surface Hodge index, CP-ahk matroid HR, CP-fh Faltings-Hriljac height, CP-af Alexandrov-Fenchel), scored by coverage with the D-H firewall as a hard side condition: "no gradient at M4" becomes "k of 5 proven cases + the firewall", and the gradient already separates the three known failure modes (genuine-m4 FULL 1.00; convex-only PARTIAL 0.60; arithmetic-blind/#40 BROKEN at CP-fq via t-blindness; too-strong/de Branges BROKEN at CP-hodge via wrong signature; off-line-forgery BROKEN). The positive mirror of oracle.py; FULL is necessary-not-sufficient (only Lean validates). **Lever B (scoped):** Lean as the non-circular value function. The keystone negDef_iff_hasseWeil (#2G-1, HodgeIndex.lean) is sorry-free, as is the formal core (Euler-Sen algebra rosatiPos_iff_hasseWeil, Class A/C no-go guards, the Frobenius cup formation guard no_dh_cupTarget = K2 as a type constraint, the digamma kernel); the GAP to an end-to-end function-field RH is the GEOMETRY (curve/divisor/intersection theory on C x C + the Frobenius graph), absent from the repo AND Mathlib, ~2-3 weeks of mostly-upstreamable formalization around (not in) the keystone. ### 97. Breadth-over-PROOF-ENGINES converges exactly like breadth-over-STATEMENTS (6b): a 5-engine sweep of signature-MANUFACTURING mechanisms is a CLEAN KILL (0 surviving bridges), a FOURTH all-roads confirmation, and it closes the from-below axis. An 11-agent workflow (~860k tokens) deployed the AI's distinctive edge (breadth of math held at once) on the one axis the cohomology landscape and transfer-search (6b) had NOT spent: matching PROOF ENGINES (HOW a theorem manufactures a (1,n-1) signature), not theorem STATEMENTS (which 6b/#46 already matched and found converged). Five from-below engines were fingerprinted against the 7-property M4 spec and run through five kill-criteria (off-line-flip / indefinite-gate / euler-archimedean / fq-shadow / k1-noncircular). ALL FIVE KILLED; anti-theater tally 5 cheap kills (coordinates), 0 surviving bridges. (1) AHK FLIP-INDUCTION (Adiprasito-Huh-Katz) is the ONE retained POSITIVE coordinate: it PASSES k1-noncircular (the Hodge-Riemann signature comes from the submodular-Lefschetz flip, never read off zeros) and fq-shadow (recovers the full Frobenius/Hodge-index machine on the realizable face), making it the unique existence-proof that a Hodge-index signature is manufacturable K1-clean with NO ambient variety (the one M4 conjunct, PROP-noncircular, with no other positive witness). It is KILLED as a finished object by off-line-flip + indefinite-gate (the Chow-ring form is t-blind, identical (1,3) for t=2 and t=100 per #40; the primitive form is positive-DEFINITE, the indefinite (1,n-1) lives only in a derived 2-plane, AHK Lemma 9.6) + euler-archimedean (atomic; the degree map yields an integer, not q+1-t). (2) Lorentzian polynomials (Branden-Huh), semismall / relative-HR (de Cataldo-Migliorini, BHMPW), and tropical / non-archimedean Monge-Ampere (Babaee-Huh, Boucksom-Jonsson) are all the AHK "too-blind" bracket in new vocabulary: unconditional, arithmetic-blind (no t-slot), cannot flip when a zero leaves the line, cannot separate zeta from D-H. (3) CKS / limit-MHS on the prismatic Sen operator (the lead bridge) is KILLED as a TRANSPORT, not a source: the nilpotent N gives the weight filtration W(N) and the Gr_l =~ Gr_{-l} duality for free, but definiteness on the primitive pieces is ALWAYS the nearby-fiber polarization Q imported through the degeneration; over Spec(Z) there is no disk to degenerate over and no Q, so importing Q IS M4. It also fails K2 (N is the archimedean Gamma/Sen structure D-H shares identically, FE/Sen residual ~1e-43 for both), and was already proven twice in-repo (the 2MM transport theorem; sorry-free SenDefiniteObstruction.lean). THE POSITIVE COORDINATE EXTRACTED: the (1,n-1) form must be SOURCED from a polarization on a real arithmetic object whose intersection numbers ARE the Frobenius trace t, never PROPAGATED from a combinatorial certificate (the Lorentzian bracket) nor TRANSPORTED through a degeneration (CKS). That is the AHK-side face of the same irreducible M4 gap whose Arakelov-side face is the Faltings-Hriljac product + Gamma_S request.

THE REFINED BREADTH DOCTRINE (the honest answer to "what is the AI edge on RH"): breadth-over-engines is now substantially SPENT and it CONVERGED, like breadth-over-statements did, so "breadth is the edge" is NOT the romantic "the AI finds the secret foreign bridge." For RH it is three honest things: (a) a CONVERGENCE DETECTOR (N distant fields collapsing to one node = near-theorem-strength irreducibility; the from-below signature-manufacturing family is now closed five ways); (b) a CORPUS COMPLETER (the sweep surfaced Boucksom-Jonsson NA-Monge-Ampere / K-stability, absent from the transfer-search corpus, then killed it as valuative at a single Berkovich place, wrong category for the archimedean continuation where the zeros sit at Re(s)<1; now added to transfer_search.py CORPUS with role='killed' and a guard test, #97 apparatus); (c) a SPEC SHARPENER. The genuinely-still-unspent axis is regime-two FRAME-AUDIT (does a distant frame collapse two of the four M4 conjuncts at once, as the acoustic reading #90-94 collapsed carries-trace into passivity); this sweep was all spec-and-fill. The sharpened AHK BUILDER target (strictly sharper than "build the surface": it asks for a lattice + Lefschetz element, NOT the variety) is recorded in [research_directions/09A_ahk_arithmetic_lattice.md](../docs/03_research/research_directions/09A_ahk_arithmetic_lattice.md): a finite graded lattice on the primes whose stars are AHK-product-structure products and whose degree/intersection map yields q+1-t over Z, carrying a t-dependent strictly-submodular Lefschetz element and an INDEFINITE (1,n-1) primitive form, positivity-on-primitive left OPEN. ### 96. A 7-candidate crazy-idea screen is a CLEAN SWEEP: all converge on the signature gap (third all-roads confirmation), and it yields a reusable front-door filter. Screened Dyson quasicrystal, Ramanujan/Ihara graphs, optimal transport / displacement convexity, max-entropy / sqrt-cancellation, arithmetic topology (knots-and-primes), free probability, and information geometry / Fisher-Rao against the three kill-criteria. Result 7/7 CONVERGES, failures partition cleanly: (K-LEVEL4) all targets are robust to moving ONE zero off-line = Level 3 (pure-point diffraction support unchanged; Alon-Boppana gap is an asymptotic lim-inf; convexity/curvature/entropy are bulk properties; free probability governs limiting spectral DISTRIBUTIONS) - the lone exception is the bare sqrt-cancellation nugget inside max-entropy, which is Level 4 but is the already-known Schoenfeld equivalence, not new. (K-DH) every candidate's only D-H separator reduces to the von Mangoldt-comb sign / Euler-product abscissa (Re>1, PNT level); none separates at the critical line; OT/Fisher-Rao are archimedean-blind (Dobner 2020: Lambda_F>=0 across all of S# including D-H). (K-R35) every positivity on offer is all-positive / trace / PSD / Bochner / Ricci>=0 / tracial - NONE carries an indefinite Lorentzian (1,n-1) signature, the only R3.5-escaping shape. Five are pre-empted in-repo (quasicrystal = e2ll/e3gg #79/#80 discrete face; Ihara = textbook, reduces to automorphic Ramanujan = GRH; knots-and-primes = Morishita reading notes, Direction-8 shadow; free probability = #76 sec 4.1 Mertens-vs-curvature ~18 kill; Fisher-Rao = Direction 12 #38/#39). THE RECURRING TRAP (identical across all 7 and the two prior kills #90-94/#95): realizing zeta as a trace/determinant/diffraction/spectrum is the easy UNIVERSAL step and IS the gap, not progress; the missing object is an INDEFINITE Hodge-index signature, so every all-positive positivity is the wrong shape by construction.

THE REUSABLE FILTER (the round's constructive byproduct): the OFF-LINE-ZERO FLIP TEST. Take a candidate's key quantity Q, inject a single hypothetical zero at 0.51+iy into the explicit-formula / spectral input, and check whether sign(Q) FLIPS. Level-4 quantities flip (the Weil form goes negative, e3c2/e3k); Level-3 quantities do not (diffraction stays pure-point, gaps stay saturated, PSD functionals stay >=0). This decides K-LEVEL4 in one local evaluation. IMPLEMENTED as [experiments/positivity/offline_flip_test.py](positivity/offline_flip_test.py) (`flip_test(Q, zeros)`): the Weil-form min-eigenvalue flips from +1.1e-2 to -2.3e-1 when one zero is moved to beta=0.8 (Level 4), while the diffraction power and pair-correlation variance are EXACTLY invariant (Level 3, functions of the heights {gamma} only, which the off-line beta-move does not touch); it even reproduces the marginal-positivity cushion in miniature (a barely-off zero beta~0.55 does NOT flip the Weil form, beta~0.8 does). Workflow: crazy-idea-screener. The standing live thread is unchanged: supply the indefinite (1,n-1) signature itself (M4 / Direction 8); stop screening all-positive re-skins.

### 95. The Lee-Yang / Ising-criticality crazy idea is KILLED (a clean coordinate). Reframing RH as "the arithmetic gas sits exactly at its Lee-Yang critical temperature" (RH <=> de Bruijn-Newman Lambda = 0, with Rodgers-Tao's Lambda >= 0 the at-or-above-critical half and RH the matching Lambda <= 0) is ELEGANT and exactly equals the marginal-positivity thesis, but it is PRE-EMPTED (Newman 1976's "RH if true is only barely so"; Newman-Wu 2019 arXiv:1901.06596 is a whole survey unifying dBN constants with Lee-Yang; already in repo #38/#12.4) and supplies NO new leverage on the missing direction Lambda <= 0. Three independent kills: (a) D-H discriminator FAILS - Dobner 2020 proved Lambda >= 0 for the entire extended Selberg class S# (no Euler axiom), which INCLUDES Davenport-Heilbronn (Lambda_DH > 0), so the dBN positivity is Euler-FREE and RH-agnostic; the Euler product enters only at the von Mangoldt comb's SIGN, a Re>1 abscissa fact that does not act on the dBN field variable. (b) LEVEL-3 mechanism: the target Lambda <= 0 is Level 4 (RH-equivalent), but every available proxy is Level 3 / RH-orthogonal - the Polya kernel Phi >= 0 is D-H-blind (suppression exp(-(pi/4)d gamma) ~ 1e-29, #38) and Newman-Wu show Phi >= 0 does not even imply real-rootedness; Rodgers-Tao's Lyapunov energy (dH/dt = -4E, E >= 0) is unconditional precisely by being RH-agnostic. (c) R3.5 / circularity: "xi is a ferromagnetic Lee-Yang partition function" is a tautology for RH (ferromagnetic => zeros on the locus => RH), and the only R3.5-escaping object (the off-shell Hodge form) is INDEFINITE Lorentzian (1,n-1), which is exactly where Lee-Yang's all-couplings-nonneg hypothesis FAILS. THE SHARP TAKEAWAY: the circle theorem zeta actually needs is the HODGE-INDEX theorem (an indefinite signature form), NOT Lee-Yang (all-positive couplings). zeta is not a literal ferromagnet. Meta: this is the SECOND crazy idea (after the acoustic thread #90-94) to reduce to the same signature gap, reinforcing the all-roads thesis - the signature really is the irreducible content.

No experiment (pure analysis workflow, leeyang-rh-step1). Cross-refs: #38 (the Polya kernel = D-H-blind soft detector, the load-bearing prior result), #12.4 (Rodgers-Tao as the RH-agnostic Level-3 half), #90-94 (the acoustic thread, the other crazy idea that converged), the all_roads thesis. Surveyor anchors: Newman 1976, de Bruijn 1950, Csordas-Norfolk-Varga, Lee-Yang 1952, Newman-Wu 2019, Rodgers-Tao 2020, Dobner 2020, Conrey-Li 1998, Knauf/primon gas. Do not re-propose; record as a closed reframing.

### 94. The Darlington construction closes the acoustic arc (EAC.5): network synthesis builds a passive system from a prescribed response, and Darlington's theorem realizes any passive impedance as a lossless 2-port + ONE resistor. The function-field weight ladder IS this network on the critical circle |s|=1 (s=alpha/sqrt q): H^0 (|s|=1/sqrt q, inside) = the RESISTOR, H^1 (|s|=1) = the lossless reactive 2-port (the zeros), H^2 (|s|=sqrt q, outside) = the SOURCE, with H^0<->H^2 paired by the FE (verified 7/7). Reactive sections are Blaschke factors; RH <=> every section degenerate (zero=pole on the circle), an off-line zero splits one into a dissipative C-section. Single-resistor insight (suggestive, not a theorem): Darlington concentrates all dissipation in ONE resistor and the arithmetic has exactly ONE archimedean place; identifying them gives the archimedean place the role of sole dissipator, all finite places lossless reactive, RH = the reactive resonances on the lossless circle. WHERE IT STALLS: zeta's Z is not rational, so the distributed Darlington = canonical system / Krein string = Suzuki's Hamiltonian-from-L, constructible unconditionally for omega>1 and lossless-for-all-omega>0 IFF RH. The construction route is real but lands on the SAME RH-equivalent gap; not a shortcut.

Net: a clarifying, unified PATH to the known target that also explains the 2/3 ceiling, the D-H discipline, and marginal positivity in one frame, and gives the archimedean place a precise role. NOT a new theorem or shortcut. Cross-refs: #90-#93, e2g, the Suzuki/de Branges prior art (surveyor, Step 1).

### 93. The reverse-engineering converges (EAC.4): the halving is POINCARE DUALITY placing the self-dual middle weight at the geometric mean of the extremes. Frobenius moduli on a curve are |H^0|=1=q^0, |H^1|=q^{1/2}, |H^2|=q=q^1, and the RH modulus q^{1/2} = sqrt(|H^0|.|H^2|) EXACTLY (verified every curve): the critical-line modulus is the Poincare-duality geometric mean of the weight-0 and weight-2 extremes, with H^1 self-dual. So the halving (exponent = weight/2, middle = 1/2) is Poincare duality + self-duality of the middle, NOT a separate positivity. In the primitive form: |Delta_0^2|=2g is q-free (diagonal/weight-0 leg), |Gamma_0^2|=2gq carries the q (Frobenius/prime leg), bound 2g sqrt(q) = their geometric mean, exponent 1/2 = average of leg weights {0,1}. Arithmetic: the functional equation xi(s)=xi(1-s) IS the Poincare duality (verified, residual ~1e-32), pairing the pole at s=1 (H^2 leg) with s=0 (H^0 leg), fixed locus Re=1/2 = the self-dual middle. zeta HAS the duality (FE) + the passive medium (Euler product); the MISSING (iii) is the weight-graded cohomology whose self-dual middle is forced to Re=1/2 = Deninger's missing motivic cohomology, with a one-line job description. D-H has no pole = no H^2 leg = no geometric mean = no halving (the geometric face of "no surface"). The acoustic reverse-engineering thus lands on the known Deninger/motivic-weight target via a NEW path that also explains the 2/3 ceiling (halving is geometric not analytic), the D-H discipline (no weight ladder without an Euler product), and marginal positivity (reciprocity saturates passivity). Convergence onto the target, not a construction of it.

Beyond here requires CONSTRUCTING the weight filtration (the open problem), not reverse-engineering its specification.

### 92. Reverse-engineering the missing M4 object one level deeper (EAC.3): RH = the passive bound HALVED. Two bounds on the resonances: PASSIVE (trivial, FREE from the Euler product) gives |alpha|<=q / no zeros right of Re=1 (exponent 1); RH gives |alpha|=sqrt(q) / Re=1/2 (exponent 1/2). So RH is exactly "halve the passive exponent 1 -> 1/2" (q -> sqrt q). In the function field this halving is the Hodge index theorem; verified on 9 curves the realized exponent log|alpha|/log q = 0.50000 exactly while passivity alone caps at <=1. Ingredient (iii), restated sharply: the energy form whose SIGNATURE halves the free passive bound. This also explains the analytic 2/3 ceiling - zero-free-region methods attempt the halving analytically and stall because it is geometric (a signature), not analytic. Two supporting clarifications: (a) ON-SHELL vs OFF-SHELL - the zeros-side Weil Gram is positive-DEFINITE (K,0) under RH (circular, on-shell, lossless), while the target (iii) is the OFF-SHELL Lorentzian (1,n-1) form on divisor classes (e2g); do not conflate them. (b) ALL-PASS mechanism - the functional equation makes the completed L a lossless all-pass for BOTH zeta and D-H; an off-line zero is a genuine 4-point all-pass quad {rho,1-rho,rho_bar,1-rho_bar}, an on-line zero degenerates to a 2-point pair on the axis, so RH <=> every all-pass section is degenerate. Passivity forbids sections only in Re>1; the strip 1/2<beta<1 is the gap (iii) closes.

### 91. The acoustic COUPLING is verified in the function field and the Spec(Z) gap is located to ONE ingredient: reading the Frobenius eigenvalues as scattering values s_i = alpha_i/sqrt(q), "passive" (max|s|<=1, = the exact Hodge margin 4g^2 q - t^2 > 0) AND "reciprocal" (min|s| = 1/max|s|, = the functional equation) FORCE "lossless" (all |s_i| = 1 = RH). The coupling needs three ingredients; over Spec(Z) zeta HAS (i) reciprocal symmetry (xi(s)=xi(1-s)) and (ii) a passive medium (Euler product, #90/EAC.1) but LACKS (iii) the Lorentzian energy form (no surface Spec(Z)^x2, no Frobenius graph, hence no signature to deliver the one-sided contractivity bound). The missing M4 polarization, restated: the object whose SIGNATURE makes the arithmetic medium provably contractive. Bonus: the passive bound is NEVER slack (max|s| = 1 exactly), because a reciprocal passive network is forced lossless. That is the marginal-positivity thesis explained: reciprocity saturates passivity, leaving no buffer for soft proofs.

### 85. The P0/P3 literature pass closes the operator-algebra leg of the LCC/BC transport in BOTH directions: the weights version of BC uniqueness exists (P0 CLOSED), the ambient Toeplitz system's KMS simplex is a point at every beta > 0 (independently re-deriving #82 Lemma 1), and NO etale groupoid has the log-crystals as its cocycle-quasi-invariant data (P3 CLOSED NEGATIVELY). [Survey](../docs/03_research/kms_weights_p0_p3_survey.md) (SURVEYOR, web-verified citations). **P0:** Christensen (arXiv:2005.01792, JNCG 2023) proves that on unital C*-algebras every KMS weight is a scalar multiple of a KMS state; composed with Bost-Connes 1995 this gives the unique KMS-beta weight RAY at 0 < beta <= 1, so the weights gap flagged in #82 (O3) is closed by citation. Stronger and more on-point: Neshveyev-Stammeier (arXiv:1912.03141, Thm 4.5) prove the multiplicative Toeplitz system T(N^x) (the dossier's actual ambient algebra) has a ONE-POINT KMS simplex at EVERY beta > 0, and their Prop 3.8 cylinder-forces the underlying measure to the flat ray: an independent literature confirmation of #82 Lemma 1. Corrections to the working picture: on Laca-Raeburn's T(N x N^x) (arXiv:0907.3760) there are NO KMS states for beta < 1 (empty at beta = 1/2, not rich), uniqueness on [1,2] is Laca-Neshveyev (arXiv:1010.0599, type III_1), richness only at beta > 2; the genuine small-beta richness example is Laca-Schulz 2025 on T(N^x x| Z) (arXiv:2503.03033, Bauer simplex on all of (0,1]). **P3:** Neshveyev's criterion (arXiv:1106.5912, J. Operator Theory 70) verified, weights extension verified (Christensen, full generality; Thomsen arXiv:1306.5118 precursor). The groupoid question resolves NEGATIVELY with two named structural reasons: the (EF) constraint is INHOMOGENEOUS (the pole term makes W_zeta an affine slice, while cocycle-quasi-invariance always cuts a homogeneous cone) and FOURIER-NONLOCAL (an atom-to-continuum oscillatory coupling, while groupoid quasi-invariance is orbit-local positive transport). So W_zeta = (groupoid KMS cone) intersect (EF slice), and ALL the rigidity lives in the slice; the "find the LCC groupoid" search is killed as a category error, not a dead end of effort.

**Reading for the program.** Combined with #82 (the C*-layer is idle in everything proven) and e3hh (the flat ray is excluded from the cone), the BC/KMS axis is now fully priced: it delivers flat-ray uniqueness (proven, three independent ways) and CANNOT deliver slice rigidity. The open core (P2a/P2b/P4) is an explicit-formula positivity problem, full stop. Constructive residue: BUILDER target B1, the dense-translate rigidity lemma on G_log = R x| Q*_+ (classify Radon measures quasi-invariant under the prime-translation action with prescribed cocycle; expected outcome: a single non-normalizable ray e^{-beta x} dx), the weights exemplar on a non-compact unit space and the cleanest remaining import candidate. Measured: organ A collapses to index 1 by `m* <= 5` on all windows (median 2, max 4 over prime windows p < 300; a Linnik-flavored least-prime-in-subgroup statistic). Organ B exhibits the phase transition at the pole: `u -> 0` stretched-exponentially at `beta = 0.7`, log-slowly at `beta = 1` exactly (`u * log p_m` stabilizes at 0.379/0.707/1.146 for M = 5/8/21), and PLATEAUS at `beta = 1.5` (ratio 0.9984 between m = 1,000 and 10,000; atom budget stalls at `1/zeta(1.5)` to 4 decimals). Amputations: removing the pole (beta > 1) or removing rotations (stream only primes = 1 mod 5: index pinned at 4 forever despite divergent `sum 1/p`) each preserves ghosts: BOTH organs are load-bearing, mirroring the #34 organ anatomy. The marginality fingerprint: at criticality the atom budget obeys Mertens, `-log(A * log p_m) -> 0.5775` vs `gamma = 0.5772`, so the #63 constant `e^gamma` reappears as the RATE of uniqueness at the pole: the simplex collapses, but barely (log-slow), which is the marginal-positivity fingerprint in the uniqueness costume.

### 80. The zeta-side moment mirror EXHIBITS the flat-extension transfer fail mode: zeta's zero-measure moment matrix never goes flat (ghost room persists ~15 orders of magnitude above the F_q floor), so the F_q composite-pinching mechanism has no purchase on zeta.

[e3gg_zeta_moment_mirror.py](positivity/e3gg_zeta_moment_mirror.py), the zeta-side follow-on to e2ll (#79). e2ll showed composite pinching holds over F_q via flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem (Curto-Fialkow): the Frobenius measure is 2g atoms, so its moment Toeplitz goes FLAT (rank stabilizes at 2g) and the representing measure / comb is UNIQUE. This experiment runs the SAME machinery on zeta's zeros to make the transfer obstruction visible. The mirror: F_q's empirical Frobenius measure `sum delta_{theta_j}` becomes zeta's empirical ZERO measure, ordinates `gamma_n` placed on the circle at `theta_n = alpha gamma_n mod 2pi`, normalized trig moments `c_k = (1/N) sum_n e^{i k theta_n}`, Toeplitz `C = [c_{i-j}]` (correlation matrix, `c_0=1`).

**The fail mode, quantitatively.** (i) F_q (flat/pinched): rank stabilizes at 2g (2 for an elliptic curve, 4 for genus-2 over F_7), ghost room (relative smallest eigenvalue) COLLAPSES to `~1e-16` at `m=2g`, normalized moments `|c_k|` are `O(1)` (atomic; the genus-2 curve gives the clean period-4 pattern `|c_k|={0,0,0,1,...}`). (ii) zeta (never flat / not pinched): rank stays FULL (`m+1` through `m=12`), ghost room PERSISTS at `0.48-0.99` (mean `~0.74`), about 15 orders of magnitude above the F_q floor; `|c_k|` are small `O(1/sqrt(N)) ~ 0.06` (the continuous limit `C -> I`), not decaying in `k`. The truncated moment problem is maximally INDETERMINATE (a continuum of ghost measures), so the moments do NOT pin the comb. Robust across circle-map scale `alpha in {0.5,1,2}`. (iii) D-H ALSO never flat (ghost room `0.46-0.93`): the continuous-spectrum obstruction is shared by every degree-1 L with infinitely many zeros, so this is a MECHANISM illustrator, NOT a zeta-vs-D-H detector.

**Takeaway.** The flat-extension uniqueness that pins the comb over F_q has no purchase on zeta: the pole-sourced CONTINUOUS archimedean spectrum keeps the moment matrix full rank (`|c_k| -> 0` as `N` grows, `C -> I`). The composite-pinching transfer cannot run through flat extension; it needs a moment-uniqueness theorem for a measure with a continuous (archimedean) component PLUS the Euler structure that singles zeta out -- LCC/EFR's named open core (#76, #79), now exhibited as a concrete ~15-order-of-magnitude ghost-room gap.

**Honest scope:** proves nothing new; repurposes the known equidistribution of zero ordinates (`sum_n e^{ik alpha gamma_n} = o(N)`, the explicit formula) to make the #79 transfer obstruction visible and quantitative. Not a detector (D-H behaves identically). A synthesis coordinate; soft-detector freeze respected (no margin, no positivity claim). Cross-refs: #79 (e2ll, the F_q side this mirrors), #76 (LCC/EFR and composite pinching), #52/#34 (the marginal wall / stealth window, the other faces of the same archimedean-continuum difficulty), #43 (the de Branges soft detector this deliberately avoids -- this is the uniqueness/rank angle, not pointwise positivity).

### 79. The function-field wind tunnel CONFIRMS composite pinching (the program's single named target) and identifies its mechanism: flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem; the transfer gap to zeta is the discrete -> pole-sourced-continuous archimedean spectrum.

[e2ll_ff_crystal_cone.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_ff_crystal_cone.py), idea 1 (the top pick) of the local-compute workaround slate ([local_compute_workarounds.md](../docs/03_research/local_compute_workarounds.md)). Tests the composite-pinching lemma shared by the session-019 survivors Lonely Crystal and Euler Facet Rigidity (#76, "a fully-saturated point of the Weil cone has no mass off the prime powers") in the one setting where RH is a THEOREM and every object is finite and exact: a curve `C / F_q`. The number field's composite structure (von Mangoldt supported on prime powers) has the exact function-field analogue `N_k = #C(F_{q^k}) = sum_{d|k} d a_d` with `a_d = #{closed points of degree d}` (the divisor sum is the composite structure; the `a_d` are the "primes"). Unitarizing the Frobenius eigenvalues, the integer power sums `s_k = sum_j alpha_j^k = q^k+1-N_k` (exact via Newton's identities on the integer zeta numerator `P(T)`) give the trigonometric moments `r_k = s_k/q^{k/2}` of the empirical Frobenius measure, and `RH for C <=> the Hermitian Toeplitz R = [r_{i-j}] is PSD <=> all angles real`, with `rank(R) = #distinct angles <= 2g`.

**Composite pinching HOLDS, on every curve.** Across 7 curves (elliptic + genus-2 over `q in {5,7,11,13}`): (a) `R` is PSD with `|alpha_j| = sqrt(q)` to `0.00e+00`; (b) the closed-point counts `a_d` recovered by exact Mobius inversion are nonnegative INTEGERS for every curve and degree (exact `Fraction` arithmetic) -- the composite/divisor-sum structure inverts with no slack; (c) Prony recovery from the moments returns exactly the `rank(R)` distinct Frobenius angles (error `0.00e+00` to `2e-16`), and the rank STABILIZES with truncation (flat extension), so the representing measure is UNIQUE -- a saturated point's mass is forced onto the closed-point/Frobenius atoms. The supersingular-type `y^2=x^5+x+1 / F_5` (`rank=2 < 2g=4`, only 2 distinct angles) is a STRENGTHENING witness: pinching holds even there, so the lemma needs no generic-angle assumption. Anti-curve control (the FF D-H discipline): planting one off-line modulus `|alpha| = sqrt(q)*1.15` makes `|r_k| > 2g` and `R` non-PSD (min-eig `-6.7`/`-5.6`), so the detector fires exactly on the RH violation.

**MECHANISM identified + the transfer gap localized.** The function-field composite-pinching IS the flat-extension uniqueness of the truncated trigonometric moment problem (Curto-Fialkow): a flat PSD Toeplitz has a unique representing measure. That is the technology LCC/EFR need over Q. The obstruction is precisely the SPECTRUM TYPE: here the spectrum is finite and DISCRETE (`2g` atoms), whereas zeta's saturated point pairs against the POLE-SOURCED CONTINUOUS archimedean spectrum, outside Curto-Fialkow / Olevskii-Ulanovskii / Bondarenko-Radchenko-Seip (which cover uniformly-discrete spectra). The wind tunnel confirms pinching where the spectrum is discrete and shows the gap is the discrete -> continuous transfer, exactly LCC's stated open core (#76), NOT the pinching idea itself.

**Honest scope:** proves nothing new about zeta; it confirms the named target is TRUE in the theorem-world, names the exact transfer technology and the exact obstruction, and rules out the cheapest kill (no ghost comb exists over `F_q`). A clean positive coordinate. Cross-refs: #76 (LCC/EFR and the composite-pinching target), #78 (EDC, the other workaround-slate test this session), #20/#54/e3x (the function-field Hodge-index/point-count machinery reused), #40 (the C-C `q->1` arithmetic-blindness, a different facet of the same FF-to-Z transfer), the D-H discipline (the anti-curve is its FF instance).

### 78. The EDC octave ladder (first test of the session-019 Eratosthenes Descent survivor) SURVIVED: the Haynsworth-telescoped self-reducing certificate has a sound skeleton, healthy per-octave margins, and NO octave-graded wall, with the global marginality emerging as the telescoped accumulation EDC predicted.

[e3dd_edc_octave_ladder.py](positivity/e3dd_edc_octave_ladder.py), idea 4 of the local-compute workaround slate ([local_compute_workarounds.md](../docs/03_research/local_compute_workarounds.md)) and the first experiment ever run against EDC (the second of the five session-019 PURSUE survivors, #76). EDC reads Weil positivity as a SELF-REDUCING certificate graded by support octaves `a_k = a_0 2^k` (here `a_0 = 0.34`, just below `log 2 / 2` so octave 0 is prime-free): the cumulative Weil Gram `G_k` (basis functions with support half-width `L <= a_k`) is a principal block of `G_{k+1}`, so Haynsworth inertia additivity gives the EXACT identity `In(G_{k+1}) = In(G_k) + In(S_k)` with `S_k` the octave Schur complement. Clause (i): every `S_k` is PSD (then telescoping gives global PSD = RH). Clause (ii): the margins `lambda_min(S_k)` decay at worst exponentially in the LEVEL `k`, not in the SUPPORT `a_k` (the latter would be the `e^{-4 pi x}` marginal wall #52 in octave clothing, voiding the certificate). Built on the M2.5/M2.6-validated blocks (`arch_block_bombieri` + `finite_block` + `pole_block`), run two ways: Part A on the genuine non-circular input-side form `M = A_arch + P_fin + B_pole` (no zeros; the actual certificate), Part B on the zero-side Gram (the 3J detector, circular but reaches higher support).

**Clause (i) CONFIRMED.** Every octave Schur complement is PSD (`In(S_k) = (+,0,0)`, `n_- = 0`) for zeta and chi_3 on the non-circular input-side form, and for zeta out to 7 octaves on the zero side. The Haynsworth identity holds EXACTLY at every octave (consistency gate passed) with `cond(G_{k-1}) <= ~90`, so the inertia counts are trustworthy. The telescoping skeleton is sound and computable.

**Clause (ii): NO octave-graded wall.** Zero-side zeta margins stay in `[0.014, 0.039]` across 7 octaves, support `b in [1.5, 2.8e9]` (a 64x span in log-support), with no exponential-in-support collapse: the smallest margin sits `~1e117` ABOVE a nominal `e^{-4 pi a_k}` wall at the top octave. Input-side zeta margins (`0.054/0.055/0.047/0.037/0.034` over 5 octaves) fit LEVEL `k` far better than SUPPORT `a_k` (`R^2` 0.98 vs 0.86). And the telescoping picture is exactly EDC's dossier prediction ("a self-reducing certificate telescopes a healthy per-octave positivity into a globally marginal one"): the global `min-eig(G_K)` is MARGINAL (`+0.007` zero side / `+0.023` input side) while every per-octave Schur margin is healthy (`O(0.01-0.05)`); the global marginality is the telescoped accumulation of healthy octaves, not any single octave being marginal.

**HONEST SCOPE (the load-bearing caveats; soft-detector freeze respected, no certified margin claimed).** (a) The zero-side flatness is nearly TAUTOLOGICAL under RH: with all zeros on the line the zero-side Gram is a sum of real rank-1 PSD outer products, so it is robustly PSD at every octave by construction; it shows scale/conditioning control, not positivity per se. The genuine test is the input side. (b) The input side (the actual non-circular certificate) CAPS at `n_oct = 5`: its prime sum runs to `n <= e^{2 a_max}` (`~5.3e4` at `a_max = 5.44`, but `~2.8e9` one octave further), so clause (ii) on the side that matters is only 4 Schur points, suggestive not decisive; that ceiling IS the feasibility wall, and whether the input-side octave margins stay bounded below as octaves grow is an ANALYTIC question, not a compute task. (c) The off-line BREAK (idea-4's promised integer discriminator, the octave-graded refinement of 3J's `schur_neg = #off-line heights`, #19) is NOT exhibited: D-H and Epstein d=47 stay fully PSD on both sides at `m = 3`/octave. This is a RESOLUTION limit, not a refutation: 3D.3 needed `K = 100-1000` to resolve D-H's off-line eigenvalues (a `-2.6%` effect), and the octave detector at `K = 15-21` is far below it; the input-side D-H PSD-ness is additionally the M2.6 stealth window (#34), and Epstein is untestable input-side (`a_1 = 0`).

**Verdict:** a falsification instrument that EDC passed. Clause (i) holds, the Haynsworth skeleton is exact and well-conditioned, and clause (ii) shows the encouraging direction (no support-wall; healthy per-octave margins telescoping into the global marginality). EDC stays a live PURSUE direction, sharpened: the open core is now precisely (b) the input-side margin lower bound past the feasibility ceiling (analytic) and (c) reproducing the off-line break at high octave resolution. The second of the three session-019 queued tests; the first (EDF, #77) also fired positive. Cross-refs: #76 (the conjecture program), #77 (EDF, the other renormalization-graded survivor EDC could supply a skeleton for), #19/3J + 3D.3 (the off-line counting law the break would refine), #52/#56 (the marginal wall the octave decomposition routes around), #34 (the stealth window D-H sits in input-side).
