The EDF under-decimation discriminator (the cheapest decisive test of the session-019 Euler Decimation Flow survivor) fired POSITIVE on both sub-tests: zeta's Weil positivity is critically tied to the full Euler product via a low-frequency mechanism, and the single-prime center-fixed-point identity is exact.

The first of the three session-019 falsification experiments ([e3aa_edf_under_decimation.py](positivity/e3aa_edf_under_decimation.py), built on the validated M2.5/M2.6 Weil-form blocks `arch_block_bombieri` + `finite_block` + `pole_block`). It tests two EDF (Euler Decimation Flow) clauses. **Part A (Clause C, the critical decimation window):** on test functions of support `[-t/2, t/2]` the "matched" Weil form `M = A_arch + P_fin + B_pole` (full prime sum, `p^k <= e^t`) stays marginally POSITIVE (min-eig `+0.029 / +0.025 / +0.020` at `t = 8/9/10`, `K=16`), but DELETING the top octave of primes (keep only `p <= e^{t/2}`, the "under-decimated" form) collapses min-eig to `-46.0 / -77.7 / -128.7`, deepening monotonically with `t`. The negative eigenvector is the BROADEST (lowest-frequency) basis mode at every `t`, confirming the predicted MECHANISM: a prime `p` resonates at frequency `xi = 2pi/log p`, so the large primes (`log p` near the support edge) resonate at LOW frequency, exactly where the archimedean kernel is most negative (`Omega(0) ~ -5.4`); they are load-bearing for low-frequency positivity, and removing them exposes the trough. **Part B (Clause B + the single-prime companion):** the angular-mean-one identity `mean_phi (1-1/p)/|1-p^{-1/2}e^{i phi}|^2 = 1` (the EDF "zeta is a center fixed point" claim) is exact to `~1e-16` for `p=2,3,5`; the `p=2` block dip depth `log2((1/2)(1-2^{-1/2})^{-2}-1) = 3.3468` matches the closed form exactly; the single-prime symbol at the resonance `xi = 2pi/log2 = 9.0647` is `-2.98`, and a genuine compactly-supported `V_8` Hann-cosine packet realizes `-1.70`, both inside the predicted band `[-3.2,-1.0]`. **HONEST SCOPE (the load-bearing caveat):** a falsification instrument that came out positive, NOT a proof or a certificate, and it respects the soft-detector freeze (no certified margin is claimed; the matched marginal positivity sits near the float64/`e^{-4 pi x}` wall, #52). Crucially, D-H ALSO goes negative under decimation (`-6.0 / -1.8 / -2.2`), so "under-decimation breaks positivity" is NOT by itself a zeta-vs-D-H separator; the clean EULER-STRUCTURED content is the center-fixed-point identity and the single-prime resonance dip, which have NO D-H analogue (D-H has no Euler product, so no per-prime block: its `-L'/L` delocalizes onto all `n`, archimedean `Omega_DH(xi) = +1.98` with no dip). The result keeps EDF a live PURSUE direction and confirms its core bet (positivity is a fixed-point property of the full Euler product, with marginality the fingerprint of a center) at the discriminator level; the open core (flow well-posedness, the Clause-E Mobius-dressing SOS witness, and turning the matched marginal positivity into a proof) is unchanged. First of the three queued tests (TODO session 019); the LCC LP triple and the ECC spectral-leakage test remain. Cross-refs: #76 (the conjecture program), #52/#56 (the marginal wall the matched form sits on), #34 (the D-H stealth window the matched D-H `+0.058` reproduces).

### 76. A nine-lens first-principles conjecture program (session 019) produced five new RH attack SHAPES that survive the D-H discipline, two clean kills, and the unifying observation that every survivor reads marginal positivity as EXTREMALITY rather than as an obstacle.

A multi-agent run (nine BUILDER lenses, one ADVERSARY pass) was asked to be creative and propose genuinely new conjectural routes to RH, then kill the ones that fail the project's own discipline (D-H instantiability, the four-level framing, K1 non-circularity, the dead-branch list). Dossier: [first_principles_conjecture_program.md](../docs/03_research/first_principles_conjecture_program.md). **Five survive (PURSUE):** Lonely Crystal (LCC, the cone of positive log-crystals for zeta's source is a SINGLETON = von Mangoldt; provable D-H firewall via the negative archimedean density at `r=0` plus no pole; open core = composite-pinching / the ghost-crystal kill); Eratosthenes Descent (EDC, octave-nested Weil positivity with the EXACT Haynsworth identity `In(G_{k+1})=In(G_k)+In(S_k)`, a self-reducing Pi^0_1 certificate where the sieve is the formation rule D-H lacks); Euler Crystal (ECC, the primes as a literal Berry-Keating scatterer crystal with the boundary condition DERIVED from the adelic product formula, distinct from the killed postulated-Hamiltonian 1C because it CONSTRUCTS the operator from local factors; open core = E3 completeness + a K1 risk in the Krein-de Branges identification); Euler Facet Rigidity (EFR, the dual of LCC in coefficient space: the saturated face of the Weil cone is `{Lambda}`, extreme rays = primes; theorem-grade D-H exclusion on three layers); Euler Decimation Flow (EDF, zeta as a CENTER fixed point of a coefficient flow, with the angular-mean-one identity explaining WHY RH is barely true, and the sharpest falsifiable predictions: critical-window exponent 1, D-H Lyapunov exponent exactly `2*beta_1-1=0.617`). **Two kills (negative coordinates):** Semicircular Polarization (free probability) is PROVEN dead by a constant: the free-convolution edge grows as `~2*sqrt(2)*log X` (Mertens) while the archimedean curvature grows as `~(1/2pi)*log T` at matched `T=X`, a ratio `~4*pi*sqrt(2)~18 >> 1`, so its central inequality is false and salvage collapses to RH itself. Adelic Ferromagnet (Lee-Yang, p-adic Teichmuller spins) is a #42 restatement: a factored product measure averages out the cross-place phase coherence and cannot reach the continuation `Re(s)<1` where the zeros live (Newman's classical no-go is the shadow). **Two reshapes:** Habiro Ladder (a Galois-Liouville `q->1` limit; load-bearing gap is the covolume `phi(N)^{1/2}` vs stealth decay `N^{-c}`, needs `c>1/2` unestablished) and Arithmetic Seifert Flow (RH as "the prime link is algebraically fibered", an INTEGER signature-flow identity; underspecified at its linking blocks and `det=|xi|^2` claim, with an unresolved amphichirality risk). **The pattern:** the `+0.035` residue and `370x` cancellation (#52/#56) become the predicted fingerprint of an extreme point, a center fixed point, or a rigid crystal, none of which can be marginally true by accident. Two high-value merges: LCC+EFR (one classification, spectral and arithmetic faces, shared composite-pinching core) and EDC+EDF (the exact Haynsworth skeleton for the richer flow). Three cheap falsification tests named (LCC LP triple `e3x_lonely_crystal_lp.py`; EDF under-decimation discriminator extending `e3c2`; ECC spectral-leakage `e1x_euler_crystal.py`). **Honest scope:** no theorem, no gap crossed; every survivor has an RH-equivalent clause (attainment / cone-membership) marked as such, and the contribution is the separable unconditional half plus the new proof topology, not a reduction in difficulty (the #73 caveat). Cross-refs: 08A M4 (the target every survivor reformulates), #42 (the wall that kills the ferromagnet), #43 (the stronger-than-RH precedent the Semicircular C4 echoed), 1C (the kill ECC routes around), #71 (the universal gap each survivor reshapes).

### 75. Two of the five no-go classes are now MACHINE-VERIFIED in Lean (sorry-free, axiom-clean): Class A (the Sen-module obstruction, UNCONDITIONAL in any positive-definite cup form via the CFC square root) and Class C (trace-blindness: no `t`-blind signature decides Hasse-Weil).

**Class A** ([lean/ZetaRH/SenDefiniteObstruction.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/SenDefiniteObstruction.lean), 5 theorems; `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` only). `skew_nilpotent_eq_zero`: a real skew-symmetric nilpotent matrix is zero, by the ELEMENTARY trace identity `tr(N^T N) = -tr(N^2) = 0` (no spectral theorem; this is the compact-group/definite-form kernel of #72/08D made into a one-screen proof). `sen_no_nilpotent_part` / `sen_nilpotent_part_not_isNilpotent`: the standard-frame Sen no-go. `bskew_nilpotent_eq_zero_of_gram`: the obstruction for any Gram factor `B = S^T S` with `S` invertible. `bskew_nilpotent_eq_zero`: the UNCONDITIONAL version, for ANY positive-definite real cup form `B` a `B`-skew nilpotent `M` vanishes, with the factor CONSTRUCTED as `S = CFC.sqrt B` (continuous functional calculus), not assumed. So `nu != 0` (Petrov) => no positive-definite invariant cup form holds for an arbitrary definite `B`, not merely the orthonormal frame. **Class C** ([lean/ZetaRH/TraceBlindObstruction.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/TraceBlindObstruction.lean), axiom-clean). `negDef_depends_on_trace`: the Hasse-Weil/Rosati `NegDef g q t` predicate is non-constant in the trace `t` (true at `t=0`, false at `t=2` for `g=q=1`). `no_trace_blind_signature`: NO predicate `P g q` of the curve data alone (with no slot for `t`) can decide `NegDef g q t`; the arithmetic-blind AHK combinatorial signature is exactly such a `t`-blind invariant (#48), so it cannot carry RH-positivity. Built against the existing `negDef_iff_hasseWeil` in `HodgeIndex.lean`. **Honest scope (both):** the LINEAR-ALGEBRA / LOGICAL kernel is machine-checked; the modeling onto the actual Bhatt-Lurie prismatic Sen operator (A) and the matroid Chow ring (C) is the paper argument (#72/#48), not formalized. Both are K5-clean CONDITIONAL no-gos: they prune the archimedean-Sen and combinatorial-signature polarization sources and relocate the demand to the Frobenius/F half; neither disproves RH. VERIFIER targets #ClassA-1, #ClassC-1.

### 74. The conditional no-go program (08E): the disprove direction is sound only as CONDITIONAL no-gos (kill criterion K5), and the deepest candidate theorem (the K2 pincer) is a COMPASS, not a wall, because the target object straddles both halves by construction.

The logical landmine: joint-realizability of the four gap properties IMPLIES RH (by the function-field template), but RH => joint-realizability is OPEN (= "does RH have a motivic/cohomological proof"). So an UNCONDITIONAL "the four are not jointly realizable" would prove RH FALSE. **K5** (new kill criterion, [08E_no_go_program.md](../docs/03_research/research_directions/08E_no_go_program.md)): a no-go is valid only if its construction class `C` is provably STRICTLY SMALLER than "all constructions" (it must name a smallness witness outside `C` and a relocation target). The ledger: Class A (source from the archimedean Sen module) = THEOREM; Class C (combinatorial AHK form) = THEOREM for fixed type; Class D (de Branges kernel) = REFUTED (Conrey-Li, positive density ~6-13%, #43); Class B (truncated determinant/trace) = STRONG EVIDENCE only (the stealth-window/soft-detector wall #18-20; the unbounded version is K5-malformed); Class E (the Frobenius/F-half ample class) = the LIVE BOUNDARY = the universal gap itself, never targeted (a full-E no-go would disprove RH). **The decisive ADVERSARY finding:** the K2 pincer (the discriminating positivity must ride the Euler/F half to separate zeta from D-H, yet must reach the off-line zeros which only the archimedean continuation reaches) obstructs the SOFT/LOCAL attack (this is WHY every soft detector failed) but NOT the target object, which straddles by construction: over `F_q` one object, the Frobenius correspondence on `C x C`, fuses `t`-carrying (its self-intersection) and zero-reaching (Rosati positivity over all eigenvalues) in a single correspondence. The straddler PROVABLY exists over `F_q`; its existence over `Z` is exactly RH. So the no-gos corral the answer from strictly inside and cannot close E. Net: "RH needs a structure not yet invented" is slightly too strong (the structure is invented in outline, the `F_q` straddler); the SOUND statement is "RH cannot be proved by any half-specific, local, combinatorial, or kernel shortcut; it requires constructing the global product straddler (`Spec(Z) x Spec(Z)` + `Gamma_S` + the arithmetic Hodge index) over Z." K5-clean throughout; nothing here disproves RH.

### 73. A purpose-built primitive system for RH plus a DuckDB lemma-graph confirm, by a third independent route (after #30 and the 15-candidate scorecard), that the 17 Spec(Z) cohomology candidates COLLAPSE onto one gap node, and the gap is the CONJUNCTION of four proven-droppable properties.

The bespoke axiomatic system ([rh_primitive_system.md](../docs/03_research/rh_primitive_system.md)) makes the SIGNATURE the primitive: the Rosati trace form `B(x,y)=Tr(x y^dagger)` is the core object, the trace `Tr` is demoted to a supporting (realization-side, D-H-buildable) functional, and RH reduces to ONE open axiom `AX-POL` (= the arithmetic Hodge standard conjecture = 08A M4). D-H-unbuildability is a property of the PRIMITIVES (no Euler product => the Frobenius algebra `A` does not exist), mirroring `lean/ZetaRH/FrobeniusAlgebra.lean`. Honest verdict: a reduction in CONFUSION, not difficulty (`AX-POL` over Z is equivalent to RH). The DuckDB lemma-graph ([experiments/lemma_db/](../experiments/lemma_db/)) operationalizes the project's epistemics as queries: a load-bearing (`depends_on`/`specializes`) vs annotation edge split, with `build_db.py` exiting NON-ZERO if any `dh_buildable` content node lies on a load-bearing path to `TGT-rh` (the D-H discipline as a CI gate). The candidate import forced all 17 candidates from [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md) into the graph and decomposed the gap node into four INDEPENDENT properties: PROP-global, PROP-carries-trace (the Frobenius/von-Mangoldt `t`), PROP-rh-equivalent (not de-Branges-stronger), PROP-noncircular (K1). RESULT: 9 realization candidates collapse onto the single gap node `TGT-m4-hodge-standard`; 4 are off-to-side proven-signature brackets each dropping EXACTLY ONE property (Faltings-Hriljac drops global, AHK drops carries-trace, de Branges drops rh-equivalent and is refuted, the Connes trace-formula family drops noncircular); 2 are pre-realization; 2 are the K1 wall. The gap is the conjunction of all four; each is individually proven-droppable; no proven object has all four; an object with all four proves RH. For the LITERAL target, global and carries-trace fuse into one datum (the product + `Gamma_S`, #70/2LO), so the two live near-misses (AHK trace-injection, FH product-construction) are two faces of the SAME object, which is precisely why the diagnosis is collapse and not a soft seam.

### 72. A compact-group theorem closes the "polarize the Sen module" branch and ANSWERS the 2KK open probe: a non-semisimple Sen operator (Petrov nu!=0) admits NO positive-definite invariant cup form, so #66 (hyperbolic) and #68 (free transport) are its ONLY non-trivial outcomes; positivity is never sourced by the archimedean Theta, only imported, = the arithmetic ample class on the Frobenius/F half (K2), = RH.

2SEN ([e2sen_polarization_obstruction.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2sen_polarization_obstruction.py), writeup [08D_sen_nonsemisimplicity_obstruction.md](../docs/03_research/research_directions/08D_sen_nonsemisimplicity_obstruction.md)) resolves the genuine Petrov question the 2KK ADVERSARY flagged as untested ("can an intrinsic positive cup form survive a non-semisimple Sen module?"). **The theorem (one line):** the Tate-equivariant cup duality `Theta^T B + B Theta = -w B` says `Theta + (w/2)I` is in `so(B)`; if `B` were positive definite then `O(B)` is COMPACT, so `so(B)` is semisimple, forcing `Theta` semisimple, i.e. `nu=0`. Contrapositive: `nu!=0` (Petrov) => NO positive-definite invariant cup form. **The trichotomy (computed, size-3 Jordan blocks, `numpy` solve of the homogeneous Sylvester):** self-paired Tate-center (`2mu=-w`) gives solution dim 2, inertia `(1,2)`, NO definite element (sign imported = #68); cross-paired (`mu1+mu2=-w`) gives dim 3, inertia `(3,3)` HYPERBOLIC (wrong polarity = #66); non-resonant gives `B=0` (the 2KK "B=0" = non-resonant placement). The semisimple control `Theta=-1/2 I_3` (`nu=0`) has a 6-dim form space CONTAINING the definite identity `(3,0,0)`; turning on `nu` collapses it to a 2-dim family with no definite element. **So #66 and #68 are the only two non-trivial outcomes, and the reason is the compact-group theorem: a definite outcome is forbidden by linear algebra the instant `nu!=0`.** Routable (monodromy-weight filtration `M(nu)`, CKS form `Q_l(x,y)=Q(x,nu^l y)` on primitives) hence NOT fatal to 08B, but the route IMPORTS the positive `Q` (no upstream VHS over `Spec(Z)` => import = RH). **K2 control in the same run (>=40 digits):** FE residual `~1.8e-43` (zeta) and `~1.1e-40` (D-H) => the Sen/archimedean/FE block is rung-(ii)-free for BOTH, so Petrov's `Theta`-obstruction is K2-blind by type; only the Euler/F half separates them (zeta `-zeta'/zeta` = von Mangoldt on prime powers; D-H `a(n)` non-multiplicative, `a(6)=+1` vs `a(2)a(3)=-0.0807`, no Euler product => polarizable algebra uninhabited for D-H by type). **Verdict:** answers 2KK = NO intrinsic positive cup form; lands at M4 as a negative coordinate that PRUNES "obtain the polarization by fixing/decomposing the Sen grading" and REDIRECTS the positivity input to an arithmetic ample/Lefschetz class on the Euler-pole `H^2` (the `F` half), which Petrov's theorem leaves untouched. K1 PASS (structural, no zeros used). Sharpens 08B step (4)(b): it must be rephrased on `M(nu)`, not on `Theta`-eigenspaces.

No recent construction supplies the global signed trace pairing carrying `t` (2P+): Tang's prismatic Poincare duality gives PERFECTNESS not the Hodge-Riemann/Rosati sign, Gurney's prismatization over Z gives the global SUBSTRATE not the cup/sign/`t`, and the Connes-Consani 2026 Jacobian gives the TRACE/realization not a signed product pairing. The three candidates are three different two-thirds of the SAME construction, each missing the sign that carries the Frobenius trace `t`.

2P+ ([2P_recent_global_signed_trace_pairing_probe.md](arithmetic_geometric/2P_recent_global_signed_trace_pairing_probe.md)) is the literature-grounded Direction 8 audit after 2LN (#69) and 2LO (#70) reduced the WCart route to one open datum: the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed trace pairing carrying `t = q+1-#E`, equivalently the product-surface correspondence `Gamma_S`. It scores the three strongest recent constructions against exactly the (iii)-polarization column (a signed pairing on the global `H^1` whose value sees `t`, not just the `(1,p)` bidegree), using the D-H discipline as a real-time classifier (a construction is RH-relevant iff it CANNOT be built for D-H: no Euler product => no Frobenius => no `t`). **Tang** (*Syntomic cycle classes and prismatic Poincare duality*, [arXiv:2210.14279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.14279), Compositio): constructs F-gauges, syntomic cycle classes, and proves perfect prismatic Poincare duality `RGamma(X)^vee ~= RGamma(X){d}[2d]` with a diagonal copairing and trace maps. Web-confirmed; no `polarization`/`Rosati`/`Hodge-Riemann`/`signed` language. This is ingredient (ii) (a perfect cup into a fundamental class) at the complex level with NO positivity: perfectness, not the sign (the entire gap per 2HH/#61). **Gurney** (*Prismatization over Z*, [arXiv:2301.12392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12392)): the global integral prismatization substrate over `Spec(Z)` with perfect cohomology under proper smooth hypotheses, but no cup form / signed pairing / trace formula carrying `t`: the substrate in which a future theorem lives, not the theorem. **Connes-Consani 2026** (*On the Jacobian of $\overline{\mathrm{Spec}\,\mathbb{Z}}$*, [arXiv:2602.15941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15941)): Picard/Jacobian monoids, spectral realization, Weil's explicit formula reinterpreted as a trace formula; the same trace-side landing every NCG framework hits (R3.5: trace positivity <=> RH, the K1 wall, #65), not a signed product pairing. **Net:** a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate. None supplies the signed `t`-carrying pairing; they stop at perfectness (Tang), the global substrate (Gurney), and the trace/realization (Connes-Consani). The post-2LO gap is unchanged and is the SAME theorem for all three: construct `Gamma_S`, form the primitive cup into the Euler-pole `H^2`, and prove the Hodge-Riemann/Rosati sign without RH input. Confirms the universal-gap reading of [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md): every candidate realizes the trace or a perfect pairing; none carries the polarization; supplying it IS RH. **Honest scope:** a literature classification (Tang web-confirmed; Gurney and Connes-Consani consistent with the repo-verified landscape entries checked against the originals); proves nothing about RH; the deliverable is the per-candidate naming of which third of the construction each lacks and that they are the same theorem. ### 70. The Euler Weil operator polar formula `C_E = A_E(-A_E^2)^{-1/2}` recovers finite-field Rosati positivity AFTER `B_E` is supplied, but cannot construct `B_E` from `(1,p)` bidegrees plus archimedean data (2LO): the bidegree `(1,q)` is shared by all curves over `F_q`, while the Frobenius trace `t = q+1-#E(F_q)` is strictly finer, so the non-circular inputs underdetermine `B_E`. The sign gap is the global Frobenius/Lefschetz signed trace pairing, equivalently the product-surface / prismatic Poincare-duality assembly.

2LO ([e2lo_euler_weil_operator.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2lo_euler_weil_operator.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2lo_euler_weil_operator.md)) is the follow-up to 2LN (#69): 2LN relocated the WCart positivity gap to the SIGN of the primitive monodromy form, fixed by the archimedean Weil operator `C_E`; 2LO tries to BUILD `C_E` from the construction target in [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md), `A_E := Omega^{-1} B_E`, `C_E := A_E (-A_E^2)^{-1/2}`. **Part 1 (function-field specialization):** over `F_q` with `B_E = [[2g,t],[t,2gq]]` and `Omega` symplectic, `A_E^2 = (t^2-4g^2 q) I`, so `-A_E^2` is positive definite iff `t^2 < 4g^2 q`; then `C_E` is an `Omega`-compatible complex structure (`C_E^2 = -I` to `7e-15`) and `Q(x,y)=Omega(x,C_E y)` is positive iff `t^2 < 4g^2 q` (206/206 rows over `g in {1,2}`, `q in {5,7,11,13}`, 0 mismatches; reduces to `HodgeIndex.negDef_iff_hasseWeil`). The polar formula WORKS. **Part 2 (the K1 audit, the crux):** the `(1,q)` bidegree is shared by every curve over `F_q`, while `t` is strictly finer; nine admissible traces over `F_5` give nine distinct `C_E`, so the bidegree + archimedean data DO NOT determine `t`/`B_E`. K1 fails CONSTRUCTIVELY: the only non-circular source of `t` is the global Frobenius point count, the product-surface / prismatic-duality assembly (Direction 8). **Part 3 (kill condition 2):** an FE/archimedean-only `C_E` forms for D-H (which has a functional equation), hence is D-H-blind and dead; the discriminating content must ride the Euler/Frobenius side. **Part 4 (K2 / D-H discipline):** the data-based `has_euler_product` guard blocks D-H and a renamed D-H; the injected off-line zero gives the 2HH/2JJ defect `|1-2 beta| ~ 0.617` (#61/#63). **Audit:** basis invariance under symplectic-compatible conjugation holds (max transform residual `2.6e-13`). **Verdict:** a sharp NEGATIVE coordinate. The polar `C_E` construction is sound and transports a supplied `B` exactly, but does not construct the arithmetic `B` geometrically; the residual M4 sign gap IS the global Frobenius trace `t`, the same object the bidegree cannot see, equivalently the product surface. Confirms the 2MM (#68) K1 burden with the precise missing piece named. **Honest scope:** finite linear algebra plus a degree-of-freedom count, not absolute prismatic cohomology; proves nothing about RH. ### 69. Petrov non-semisimplicity is ROUTABLE but UNHELPFUL on the primitive quotient (2LN): the defective Sen nilpotent dies on the monodromy primitive quotient by Jacobson-Morozov, so non-semisimplicity is NOT the wall, but the positivity gap relocates undiminished to the SIGN of the primitive monodromy form `Q_k`, which the archimedean Weil operator `C_E` (not `N`) fixes. The direct Hermitian Rosati/Lyapunov metric fails on the full nonsemisimple block yet exists on the semisimple quotient; the missing datum is the geometric Frobenius/Lefschetz operator supplying the primitive decomposition, not `Theta`.

2LN ([e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ln_wcart_primitive_quotient.md), proposal [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md)) is the only WCart direct-polarization escape hatch left after #67/#68. It builds a faithful defective Sen model from `sl2` irreps (genuine Jordan blocks, fixing the 2KK defect where the "Jordan block" collapsed to a relabeled diagonal): `N` the lowering operator, `Theta = -1/2 I + N` at the Tate center, the cup form `Omega` solving `Theta^T Omega + Omega Theta = -Omega` (residual `0`), the monodromy weight filtration `W(N)`, and the primitive forms `Q_k(x,y) = c_E * Omega(x, N^k y)`. **Part 1 (the 2LM direct metric):** the Hermitian Rosati/Lyapunov metric `Theta^* H + H Theta = -H, H > 0` is feasible iff `A = Theta + 1/2 I = N` is similar to skew-Hermitian (diagonalizable, purely imaginary spectrum); a genuine nilpotent is not diagonalizable (eigvec cond `inf`), so NO positive `H` exists on the full block. **Part 2 (Jacobson-Morozov erasure):** the same-weight endomorphism action of `N` on the primitive graded quotient is `0.0e+00` (N lowers weight by 2, hence the zero endomorphism on each graded piece), and the Lyapunov metric on the semisimple quotient operator IS feasible. So the primitive quotient erases the Petrov nilpotent for free. **Part 3 (the residual gap is the sign):** `Q_k` is `+definite` for `c_E=+1` and `-definite` for `c_E=-1` with identical `|defect|`; the polarization sign is unconstrained by `N`, governed by the archimedean `C_E`, and IS the unmoved M4 / arithmetic Hodge-standard-conjecture gap. **Part 4 (Outcome-3 control):** with a misaligned (non-canonical) nilpotent `L` the Petrov nilpotent SURVIVES inside `ker L` (`||N|ker|| ~ 0.29` vs `0` for the `N`-aligned choice), isolating the missing datum as the geometric Frobenius/Lefschetz operator, not `Theta` alone. Robust on block patterns `(3,)`, `(4,)`, `(2,2)`. **Audit A-E all pass:** sign convention (`-Omega` is the cup), pure cup symmetry type matching block parity, derivation nullspace dim equals the Tate-dual Jordan matching count `sum_{a,b} min(m_a,m_b)`, basis invariance under random conjugation, and the D-H discipline (formation guard blocks D-H; an injected off-line zero at `beta ~ 0.8085` gives polarization defect `|1-2 beta| ~ 0.617`, reproducing #61/#63). **Verdict:** WCart stays ALIVE (the direct route is not closed by non-semisimplicity), but the primitive quotient buys NO positivity; the next task is geometric (construct the Frobenius/Lefschetz operator and the archimedean `C_E` sign), exactly the #68 M4 burden restated on the primitive quotient. **Honest scope:** finite linear algebra on a faithful `sl2`/Jordan model, not absolute prismatic cohomology; the erasure is structural (Jacobson-Morozov) and the sign is supplied as a phase, not derived; proves nothing about RH. A sharpening NEGATIVE-as-coordinate that resolves the Petrov question and relocates the gap. Cross-refs: #66 (2KK wrong polarity, which asked for higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star positivity), #67 (2LL survival), #68 (2MM transport / K1 burden), #61/#63 (the `0.617` polarization defect), 08A M4 / [spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md](../docs/03_research/spec_z_cohomology_landscape.md) (the universal polarization gap).

Importing `B` from a Weil form or known zeros would be K1-circular.

2LL ([e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2ll_euler_sen_polarization.md), proposal [euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md](../docs/03_research/euler_sen_polarization_attempt.md)) executes the first falsification attempt against the new Euler-Sen proposal. Model: for the function-field/Rosati matrix `B_E(g,q,t) = [[2g,t],[t,2gq]]`, build `H = P_top + N(P_top)`, `N(top_i)=lower_i`, `Omega(top_i,lower_j)=B_E[i,j]`, and `Theta=-1/2 I+N`. This is genuinely non-semisimple (`rank N=2`, `N^2=0`, geometric/algebraic multiplicity `2/4`) and still satisfies the cup-target derivation equation exactly (`Theta^T Omega + Omega Theta = -Omega`, residual `0.000e+00`) because the defective block sits at the Tate center. **Result:** the primitive monodromy form `top^T Omega N top` equals `B_E`; across `q={5,7,11}`, genus `1`, and integer traces around the Hasse-Weil window, positivity matches `t^2 < 4g^2q` with `0` mismatches. Representative `q=5`: `t=4` gives primitive signature `(2,0,0)` positive, `t=5` gives `(1,1,0)` indefinite; the 2KK-style diagonal-star baseline is rigidly hyperbolic `(2,2,0)` in both cases. **Verdict:** SURVIVES AS FORMALISM, NOT AS PROOF. It shows the right operation is monodromy primitive polarization, not a diagonal Hodge-star on the cup form, and it recovers the known function-field Rosati/Hasse-Weil sign after the 2G sign flip. **Critical caveat:** `B_E` is supplied as input; defining `B_E` geometrically over `Spec(Z)` from Euler/Frobenius data and the archimedean metric, without zero-side or RH input, remains exactly the M4 gap. K1 risk is therefore live, not discharged. K2 is represented by a data guard (`has_euler_product=False` blocks formation), now aligned with the Lean `FrobeniusAlgebra.CanFormCupTarget` guard, but still toy-level.

### 66. The WCart Hodge-star route to organ (a) is WRONG POLARITY at rank 2 (2KK): the trial Hodge-star polarization of the alternating cup form on the truncated diffracted Hodge complex is unconditionally hyperbolic (signature (1,1) on its rank-2 support) for every Hodge-star phase and every K in 2..32, so it can never flip to flag an off-line zero (the AHK pattern #48 again). A real RH-relevant polarization needs higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star (Frobenius-weighted trace) positivity.

2KK ([e2kk_wcart_finite_model.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2kk_wcart_finite_model.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2kk_wcart_finite_model.md)), built by a BUILDER agent and then re-run and audited by an ADVERSARY agent during the "where do we go from here" deliverables workflow (2026-06-04). It is the FIRST WCart probe to actually form the cup pairing as a matrix and read its signature (2PR.1/2DD only located the trace-halves as determinants). Model: a finite truncation of the Bhatt-Lurie diffracted Hodge complex, graded weights n=0..K, Sen operator Θ acting by -n (BL 3.5.8); the alternating Θ-compatible cup form B solved from the Sylvester equation Θ^T B + B Θ = -B (H^2 = C(-1), Tate weight -1); trial polarization Q = B^H · (Hodge-star phase i^w). **Robust result:** the signature is (1,1,K-1) across all four Θ rows and stable for every phase and every K (2..32) -- unconditionally hyperbolic on the rank-2 support, hence wrong polarity (like the convex-Hodge/AHK signature #48, it cannot detect an off-line zero). **The defensible takeaway:** a rank-2 alternating cup on a SINGLE weight pair is hyperbolic by linear algebra alone; organ (a) therefore cannot be carried by a Hodge-star on the obvious cup form, and a genuine RH-relevant polarization needs either higher-rank primitive cohomology or a non-Hodge-star (Frobenius-weighted trace) positivity. **Methodological catch (ADVERSARY, three defects, all flagged in the .py STATUS banner and the .md correction blocks):** (1) Gate 1 "D-H non-formation" is a STRING MATCH on L.name, not a structural test -- the cup form is built from Θ(K) alone, so the L-function never enters the matrix and zeta/D-H would coincide if both reached the solver (the K2 exclusion is hand-imposed, correctly motivated but not discovered by the model); (2) the Petrov non-semisimplicity crux (arXiv:2302.11389, the actual content of the probe) is NOT exercised -- theta_jordan collapses to a relabeled diagonal, no genuine defective Jordan block is built, and a real one DESTROYS the cup form (B=0); (3) the off-line polarity probe is INVALID -- the off-line Θ has no exact null vector, so the solver silently returned a non-cup-form (derivation residual 1.82 vs 0.0 on-line). **Honest scope:** the wrong-polarity conclusion is solid as linear algebra on a rank-2 truncation, NOT a theorem about absolute prismatic cohomology; it closes the cheap Hodge-star finite model, not the real object. Retained as a recorded NEGATIVE coordinate (do not re-run; do not cite the three broken gates). The genuine open question Petrov poses -- can an intrinsic positive cup form survive a non-semisimple Sen module -- remains untested and is the next honest WCart probe if the route is pursued.

### 65. The soft-max de-idempotentization does NOT restore the Frobenius trace t on the Connes-Consani square, so the C-C signed-pairing handle on Direction 8 is closed; the better handle for milestone M4 is the Bhatt-Lurie WCart substrate (Frobenius F + Sen Θ on one stack).

2CC.4 ([e2cc4_adversary_probe.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc4_adversary_probe.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2cc4_adversary_probe.md)), generated and run by an ADVERSARY agent during the "where could we move the needle" workflow (2026-06-04), then reviewed and re-run by hand. Tests the #42 follow-up speculation that the finite-β soft-max (which removes the characteristic-1 idempotency, scalar defect log2/β ≠ 0) could, carried into the bilinear form, restore the trace t the β=∞ mixed-volume form froze at Δ·Γ = p−1 (vs the function-field target Δ·Γ = q+1−t, t roaming the Hasse-Weil window). **Three honest readings** of "soft-deform the pairing": R1 (soft|det| in each Gram entry), R2 (smooth the polygon corners, recompute exact Euclidean mixed area), R3 (is there ANY free knob at fixed p?). **Result:** both R1 and R2 recover the frozen p−1 at β=∞; at finite β, Δ·Γ(β,p) is a SINGLE deterministic number per (β,p) with no independent parameter (R1 deviations vanish as p grows -- pure scalar softening; R2 deviations are the perimeter/area inflation, monotone in p). No reading ever roams the Hasse-Weil window at fixed p. **Verdict: t is NOT restored.** The combinatorial area pairing is a determinant |det(u,v)| of lattice direction vectors, which has no t to begin with: t is a point count q+1−t, strictly finer than the (1,p) bidegree (#25), and four genus-1 curves over F_5 share identical slope data. Soft-max cannot refine a bidegree into a point count. **Consequence:** closes the soft-max route to the missing signed pairing for the whole C-C-square family, which is why the needle-map routes M4's most attackable handle to WCart (F = Euler/von Mangoldt half, Θ = Γ-divisor and a proven derivation, cohomological dimension 1, Tang's prismatic Poincaré duality to build on), not the C-C signed pairing. **Honest scope:** R1/R2 decisive computations on the divisor shadow (the e2cc machinery), R3 a degree-of-freedom count; falsifies the SPECIFIC soft-max mechanism, not every conceivable de-idempotentization; proves nothing about RH; a pruned Tier-2 handle.

### 64. RH is a Π⁰₁ sentence, so undecidability is a BACK DOOR to truth, not an escape: a proof that RH is independent of ZFC would itself prove RH true. The Lean substrate now carries the Π⁰₁ kernel witness, and the need for ever-sharper effective bounds at the tight (highly composite) n -- with no uniform soft bound covering all n -- is the formalization-layer shadow of marginal positivity.

A logical-status excursion (doc [rh_logical_status.md](../docs/03_research/rh_logical_status.md), Lean [RHEquivalences.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/RHEquivalences.lean)), adversarially verified by two multi-agent workflows. **The lever (PROVEN):** RH is provably equivalent to a Π⁰₁ arithmetic sentence (Robin 1984 / Lagarias 2002 / the DPRM Diophantine form), so (i) a false RH has a finite checkable counterexample, hence is refutable already in Robinson arithmetic Q -- the engine is Σ₁-COMPLETENESS, not Σ₁-soundness (the usual misattribution of the slogan); (ii) therefore irrefutability ⟹ truth, so "prove RH undecidable" is not a third outcome beside true/false: succeeding at it would PROVE RH true, in a metatheory forced to be Con(ZFC)-strong by Gödel II. (iii) Forcing provably cannot show RH independent (it does not change ω, hence not arithmetic truth); large cardinals CAN prove new Π⁰₁ truths so could in principle prove RH, but cannot exhibit it as two-sided independent. The dead corners (independence-as-a-program, Chaitin "true for no reason" which Chaitin himself disclaims, busy-beaver state-count records, program-size bit counts) carry no metamathematical content; the only genuinely open technical core is the reverse-math strength of the zero-counting step N(T) (bracketed WKL₀-up-to-ACA₀). **Lean (8 sorry-free anchors, all `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`):** RHEquivalences.lean defines Robin/Lagarias/Mertens/Li-Keiper/Nyman-Beurling as Props over `zeta` and `RH_arith := lagariasInequality` (the Π⁰₁ surrogate); proves the definitional iff, the Mathlib bridge, `rh_arith_refutable` (¬RH_arith ↔ ∃ one refuting n -- the Σ⁰₁ "no false-but-unrefutable" dual made formal), kernel-computable σ at 6/12, and the Lagarias inequality at n=1 (equality, the reason the criterion is ≤ not strict <), n=3 (order-1 effective bounds: e^{11/6}≥e·(11/6), log(11/6)≥5/11), and n=2 (the hardest small case, needing integer-vs-rational power comparisons e^{3/2}≥4 from (e^{3/2})²=e³=(e¹)³≥(27/10)³, log(3/2)≥2/5 from (e^{2/5})⁵=e²≤(68/25)²≤(3/2)⁵). **The marginal-positivity shadow (corrected):** n=2 needs sharper bounds than n=3 because its margin (0.32) is smaller than n=3's (1.62) -- the margins are NON-monotone in n (0, 0.32, 1.62, 0.98, 4.38 for n=1..5), and the genuinely tight cases are highly composite n (n=12 margin ~0.01, where σ(n) is large). No UNIFORM soft bound covers all n, because that would be proving RH; that, not a spurious "escalating with n" trend, is the real formalization-layer trace of marginal positivity. **Honest scope:** the five deep equivalences (Robin/Lagarias/Mertens/Li/NB ⟺ RH) are documented sorries (#RB-1/#LG-1/#MT-1/#LI-1/#NB-1), none dischargeable without proving RH; this is a SIDE excursion that sharpens the target (no undecidability escape, soft arguments structurally barred, reinforcing the Level-4 commitment) but supplies no polarization. Distinct from ExplicitFormula.lean's Weil-form criterion (#EF-2).

The duality-vs-polarization defect D(gamma)=|1-2beta| is a D-H-AWARE detector: identically 0 for zeta (25 zeros, RH) and a 0.617 SPIKE at D-H's off-line zero gamma~85.7, flat on D-H's 35 on-line zeros. The #34 stealth window is a RESOLUTION COST (resolving height gamma needs primes to e^gamma; e^{85.7}~1e37), NOT intrinsic blindness: the exact form is D-H-aware, only the reachable reconstruction is blind.

Using 2HH's (#61) handle, the defect D(rho)=|（1-rho)-rho-bar|=|1-2beta| as a function of HEIGHT is the D-H-aware detector. **Results:** Part 1: D(gamma) identically 0 for zeta (25 zeros to T=90, all on line); for D-H, 0 on its 35 on-line zeros (to T=60) and a SPIKE to 0.617 at the off-line zero gamma~85.7 (beta~0.8085) -- the detector LOCATES the off-line obstruction, does not fail to see it. Part 2: the stealth window (#34) is a RESOLUTION cost -- resolving height gamma via the non-circular prime side needs test functions of frequency ~gamma, hence primes up to e^gamma; e^{85.7}~1.6e37 primes, unreachable, while reachable b<=6 resolves heights only to log(b^2)~3.58 (below the first zeta zero at 14.1). So the EXACT form (from zeros) sees the off-line zero for free; the reconstruction provably cannot. The stealth window is the e^gamma cost, NOT intrinsic blindness of the Weil form to D-H. Part 3: the reframed discipline -- OLD "is it silent on D-H?" -> NEW "does it give each L its TRUE zero locus?"; the exact Weil form / D(gamma) passes (D-H-aware: spikes at D-H off-line, flat for zeta), the cohomological reason being 2HH/2GG (on the line FE-partner = conjugate = cup product is a polarization; D-H's off-line zero is where that fails). **Verdict:** the stealth window is a TARGET, not a wall; the D-H discipline upgrades from D-H-EXCLUDED to D-H-AWARE; the right object gives D-H its off-line zeros and zeta its on-line zeros from one mechanism (cup-is-a-polarization, 2HH). **Honest scope:** D(gamma) is computed from the actual zeros (a localization demonstration, circular as a proof); the resolution argument is quantitative and non-circular; nothing proves RH. The reframe is the deliverable.

So the qualitative incommensurability 2FF/#59 used (organ (b), "no single period") is FREE, not a deep transcendence statement; candidate F's deep version would need an EFFECTIVE (Baker) bound or independence with the archimedean periods. **Results:** Part 1: smallest |sum a_i log p_i| (p<=11) shrinks 0.0465 -> 0.0019 as |a_i| grows 1->3 but stays >0 (one-line proof). Part 3 (controlled sensitivity): rebuilding the prime block with sampling positions {log n} snapped to a commensurable grid degrades min eig(M) (+0.026 -> -0.39 at grid 0.10) but NO MORE than an equal-RMS incommensurable random perturbation (-0.33 +/- 0.34, n=12): commensurability z-score -0.20 (and -0.67 at grid 0.20), nowhere near -2. The large control variance shows it is displacement MAGNITUDE, not commensurability, that moves positivity. **Verdict:** candidate F is a WEAKER bridge than hoped; the independence it supplies is elementary, the margin sits above the effective-gap scale, and positivity is not specifically resonance-sensitive. The 2FF organ-(b)<->F bridge connects organ (b) to a FREE fact, importing no Baker machinery. Refines #59: the obstruction to a single period IS the independence of {log p} (true) but that independence is elementary, so the bridge is real and SHALLOW. **Net consolidation:** the deep content concentrates on the H^2 / Euler-product side (organ (a), 2GG/2HH), away from the transcendence side -- a clean negative that prunes a hoped-for import and sharpens where the work is. **Honest scope:** Part 1 elementary; Part 2 a scale comparison (gaps shrink with more primes/coeffs, so "above the gap scale" is for the reachable regime); Part 3 a controlled truncation-level sensitivity test, not a theorem. Locates where candidate F lives; not a statement about RH.

### 61. Brick 1 / organ (a) made exact: RH <=> the Poincare-duality cup product is a POLARIZATION <=> FE-partner (1-rho) = conjugate (rho-bar) <=> Re=1/2. PERFECTNESS is free (the FE gives a perfect cup-product duality even for D-H, residual 6e-30); POSITIVITY is the gap (D-H's off-line zero displaces FE-partner from conjugate by |1-2beta|=0.617). The defect is a FIXED structural quantity (stealth-free as a STATEMENT), but its non-circular evaluation is still the analytic gap.

2HH ([e2hh_cup_is_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2hh_cup_is_polarization.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2hh_cup_is_polarization.md)) executes brick 1 of state_of_candidate_ABF (organ (a), the prismatic Poincare duality) and answers its perfectness-vs-positivity question. **The characterization:** a polarization = a duality compatible with a positive Hermitian structure (Hodge-Riemann). On H^1 the cup product (Poincare duality, from the FE) pairs rho with 1-rho; the Hermitian polarization (Hodge star / conjugation) pairs rho with rho-bar; they coincide iff 1-rho=rho-bar iff Re(rho)=1/2. So RH <=> the cup product IS a polarization <=> FE-partner = conjugate for every zero. **Results:** (1) zeta: first 10 zeros all beta=1/2 exactly, defect |1-2beta|=0 (cup IS the polarization, perfect AND positive). (2) D-H: (a) FE residual 6e-30 -- D-H HAS the duality, so the cup product is a PERFECT pairing for D-H too (perfectness is NOT the discriminator); (b) off-line zero rho~0.8085+85.699i (verified, |L|=1.1e-5) has FE-partner 1-rho~0.1915 DIFFERENT from conjugate rho-bar~0.8085, displacement 0.617 -- the cup product is NOT a polarization for D-H (positivity fails, perfectness holds). (3) the displacement 0.617 is a FIXED structural quantity, not a reconstruction-floor quantity: unlike the stealth window (#34) it does not shrink with truncation, visible at any resolution including the off-line zero. CATCH (honest): evaluating it needs the zero location (circular) OR the non-circular reconstruction (back under the stealth floor) -- so the CHARACTERIZATION is exact and stealth-free, its NON-circular evaluation is the analytic gap. **Verdict:** POSITIVITY, not perfectness, is organ (a)'s gap; the open content is "the FE-duality is Hodge-Riemann positive" = RH, on the infinite-dim H^1, importing the standard-conjecture frame on the H^2/duality face. Combined with 2GG (#60): D-H fails twice (no nonzero H^2 unit; AND FE-partner != conjugate), both saying its duality is not a polarization. **Lean:** added typed placeholder targets to PrismaticCohomology.lean (cup_product, fundamental_class, Q3a_fundamental_class_nonzero, Q3b_cup_is_polarization_iff_RH) in the existing Unit/True-sorry idiom; NOT compiled this session (no toolchain), to be verified on the owner's build. **Honest scope:** an exact elementary characterization demonstrated on real zeros (not a non-circular certificate); proves nothing new about RH; a sharpening coordinate.

### 60. Organ (a) of M4 (the H^2 fundamental class): the functional equation IS the Poincare duality H^1 x H^1 -> H^2, but D-H HAS a functional equation too, so the DUALITY (pairing) is buildable for D-H. What separates is the FUNDAMENTAL CLASS H^2 = the Euler-product pole (zeta residue 1; D-H entire, residue 0). Organ (a)'s obstruction is the nonzero UNIT, not the pairing -- the cleanest cohomological statement of the D-H discipline: D-H has H^1 x H^1 -> H^2 but H^2 = 0, so its duality is not a polarization.

2GG ([e2gg_fundamental_class.py](arithmetic_geometric/e2gg_fundamental_class.py), writeup [.md](arithmetic_geometric/e2gg_fundamental_class.md)) attacks organ (a) named by probe B (#58): the fundamental class H^2 and the trace map H^1 (x) H^1 -> H^2 = unit. **The decisive point:** the FE xi(s)=xi(1-s) (with xi = (1/2)s(s-1)pi^{-s/2}Gamma(s/2)zeta(s)) IS the Poincare duality, pairing rho with 1-rho, and the s(s-1) factor carries the Deninger grading s=0 (H^0), s=1 (H^2, the pole = fundamental class, weight-2 Tate twist), nontrivial zeros (H^1). But D-H ALSO has a FE (its defining property), so the duality pairing is buildable for D-H. What D-H lacks is the NONZERO fundamental class: zeta has a pole at s=1 (residue 1, the Euler-product divergence prod_p (1-1/p)^-1 -> inf), D-H is ENTIRE (residue 0). **Results.** Part 1 (FF anchor, real): the cup product H^1 x H^1 -> H^2=Q(-1) is a PERFECT (nondegenerate, alternating) Poincare pairing across the genus 1-2 family, Frobenius is a similitude of scale q (Phi^T J Phi = qJ), and the Riemann polarization is definite <=> |alpha|=sqrt q -- over F_q BOTH organ-(a) ingredients present. Part 2 (the separation): zeta FE holds to 5e-36 (duality); zeta residue at s=1 = 1.000000 (nonzero H^2, the Euler pole); D-H residue = 9.2e-11 ~ 0 (entire, no H^2), YET D-H has its own FE. Decisive contrast: fundamental class 1 vs 0. **The DUALITY (FE) does NOT distinguish zeta from D-H; the FUNDAMENTAL CLASS (Euler pole) DOES.** Organ (a)'s obstruction is the nonzero unit, not the pairing -- K2 in cohomological language: D-H has the pairing but H^2 = 0, so its duality is not a polarization (the cleanest cohomological form of the D-H discipline yet, and it grades probe B's rank-1 ample direction as the s=1 H^2). Part 3 (named gap): present = the FE-symmetry (even for D-H) + the rank-1 Euler-pole H^2 (2EE); MISSING = a genuine GEOMETRIC Poincare duality realizing H^1 (x) H^1 -> H^2 as a PERFECT CUP PRODUCT into the Euler-pole fundamental class on the INFINITE-dim arithmetic H^1 (the prismatic Poincare duality, candidate A's dependency (i)), whose induced polarization positivity is RH. **Honest scope:** Part 1 rigorous (reproduces 2T/2G/2DD in duality language); the residue contrast is a clean decisive computation; the H^0/H^1/H^2 grading + "FE = duality, pole = fundamental class" is the Deninger picture, NOT a constructed cohomology, proving nothing about RH. A sharpening coordinate.

### 56. The M2.6 stealth window is a HUGE-CANCELLATION residue, and the archimedean block is NOT a cushion (it is indefinite). The three input-side blocks of the Weil/Rosati form have norms 55 (A_arch), 69 (P_fin), 123 (B_pole) but their sum has norm 0.33: positivity for zeta is the residue of a ~370x cancellation, NOT "a positive archimedean cushion dominating an indefinite prime perturbation." A_arch is itself indefinite (min eig -55; Omega(0) < 0), correcting a false claim in the e3m docstring. The stealth window follows mechanically: the off-line obstruction (~2.6% of the tiny net, absolute ~0.05) sits below the reconstruction residual of the huge canceling blocks (~10% of the net, absolute ~0.19), ratio 0.25 < 1, so it is invisible to any input-side certificate at reachable K.

Calibrated exactly against e3m (reconstruction residual 0.078 for zeta, recon rel-min +0.0805).

**Computed (K=10, b in [1.3,6]).** Blocks (norm, min eig): A_arch (55.3, -55.2), P_fin (69.0, -68.9), B_pole (123.2, +0.0 to +123.2, rank-1 from the s=1 pole), M = A+P+B (0.33, +0.019), M_zero (0.31, +0.013), reconstruction residual 0.078. Cancellation ratio max||block|| / ||M_net|| = 372x. By Weyl, A_arch + P_fin alone has min eig <= -52; only the large positive pole block lifts the sum to the tiny positive M_zero. D-H: net norm 1.86, zero-side rel-min +0.011 (off-line obstruction hidden = spuriously positive), input-side rel-min +0.044, residual 0.104; off-line absolute ~0.048 vs residual absolute ~0.194 (ratio 0.25). The cancellation ratio is large and roughly stable across K in [6,12] (348-429x, not strongly monotone in this reachable range); the true infinite-resolution margin is the e3v doubly-exponential wall e^{-4 pi x} (#52).

**The finding.** This is the mechanism behind the M2.6 stealth window (#34) and a correction: (1) the e3m "A_arch is manifestly positive-semidefinite" claim is FALSE (A_arch min eig -55; the symbol Omega is negative near t=0), corrected in both e3m and e3y. (2) The marginal-positivity thesis gets its sharpest input-side form: the positive Weil form is the ~370x-cancellation residue of three large blocks, so its sign is buried under the reconstruction error of those blocks, which is precisely why no input-side (non-circular) certificate can see D-H's off-line zeros at reachable K. (3) It confirms the correct off-line detector is the ANSWER-side Schur complement (e3j), which works in the zero basis and is not limited by input-side reconstruction error. The session-long seam thesis (off-line content in the prime/Euler block, archimedean shared with D-H) is consistent: here the archimedean AND prime blocks are both large and indefinite, and the off-line obstruction is a small fraction of the net residue, below the reconstruction floor.

**Honest scope.** A clean float64 computation calibrated exactly against e3m (same blocks, same residual). The cancellation ratio and the indefiniteness of A_arch are robust facts; the "deepens with K" reading is weak (the ratio is ~400x and roughly flat in K in [6,12], not strongly monotone), so the entry claims only that the ratio is large and that the true margin is the e3v wall. The "2.6% off-line fraction" is imported from #18/3D.3, not re-derived here. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; it is the mechanism of the stealth window and a docstring correction. ### 55. The Bost-Connes multiplicativity obstruction (overnight C4): D-H provably has no equilibrium product state. The pointwise multiplicativity defect d(L) = mean |a_{mn} - a_m a_n| / mean |a_m a_n| over coprime pairs is 0 for Euler products (zeta, chi3; the KMS equilibrium is a pure product state (x)'_p rho_p) and > 0 for non-Euler functions (D-H 1.16, Epstein-d47-principal 5.14). Because d > 0 for BOTH the RH-false (D-H) and the RH-true (Epstein) non-Euler controls, the obstruction detects NON-EULER-ness, not RH-failure: it is the Bost-Connes form of the K2 firewall, necessary-not-sufficient. The algebraic twin of e3w's analytic loglog-coefficient (#53).

Overnight cycle C4 ([e3z_multiplicativity_obstruction.py](positivity/e3z_multiplicativity_obstruction.py)). An Euler product <=> a_{mn} = a_m a_n for gcd(m,n)=1 <=> the Bost-Connes partition function factors Z(beta) = prod_p Z_p(beta) <=> the beta>1 KMS state is the restricted tensor product (a pure product state). A sum of two distinct primitive L-functions (D-H = c1 L(chi1) + c2 L(chi2)) is a convex MIXTURE of the constituents' product states, a product state iff the constituents coincide.

**Computed (coprime m,n>=2, mn<=400, 486 pairs).** d(zeta) = d(chi3) = 0 to machine precision; d(D-H) = 1.16; d(Epstein-d47-principal) = 5.14. Sample: D-H has a_2 a_3 = -0.08 but a_6 = 1.0 (defect 1.08); Epstein has a_2 a_7 = 0 but a_14 = 4 (defect 4). 

**The finding.** This makes concrete the construction sweep's Mechanism-2 claim (#53) and sharpens the 2A_R1 D-H exclusion from the prose "D-H is a linear combination, not a geometric object" to the operator-algebra statement "D-H has no equilibrium product state at all." It is a clean, non-circular, single-number, D-H-discriminating invariant (the algebraic counterpart of the analytic loglog-coefficient #53), and it lands in the SAME place: necessary-not-sufficient (the Epstein trap, #20/#27). Two independent invariants (analytic density #53, algebraic multiplicativity #55) now confirm the seam from the construction sweep: multiplicativity fixes the object's EXISTENCE (product state / Euler factorization), but the off-line-zero content lives in the shared archimedean continuation, so neither invariant can be promoted to a positivity that implies RH.

**Honest scope.** A direct, exact computation (the defects are algebraic, no approximation). The Bost-Connes / KMS interpretation is standard (Bost-Connes 1995); the new content is the quantified defect across the project's controls and the explicit Epstein trap confirming necessary-not-sufficient. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; a K2-firewall sharpening. Cross-refs: #53 (the construction sweep + the analytic loglog twin), #20/#27 (the von-Mangoldt fingerprint / Epstein trap family), #46 (the Euler block carries the sign), 2A_R1 (the D-H exclusion this sharpens). References: Bost-Connes 1995 (KMS / product states); reuses the shared dirichlet_coefficient interface.

### 54. The function-field polarization, made concrete (the missing object where it is a theorem). Built the intersection form on NS(E x E) for 23 elliptic curves over F_p: signature is exactly (1,3) for EVERY curve (Hodge Index Theorem), the primitive part (orthogonal to the ample h+v) is negative-definite, and the RH-for-E bound a^2 <= 4q holds with a HEALTHY buffer 4q - a^2 = O(q) (median 2.88 q). This exhibits exactly what the integer case is missing: over F_q the polarization is automatic with an O(q) buffer; over Z the analogue Weil form has only the e^{-4 pi x} marginal buffer (#52) AND the surface Spec(Z) x Spec(Z) carrying the Frobenius class is not even constructed.

Overnight cycle C1 ([e3x_function_field_polarization.py](arithmetic_geometric/e3x_function_field_polarization.py)). On S = E x E the classes h (horizontal), v (vertical), Delta (diagonal), Gamma (graph of q-Frobenius) have intersection numbers h.v = h.Delta = v.Delta = h.Gamma = 1, v.Gamma = q, Delta.Gamma = #E(F_q) = q+1-a (all self-intersections 0 by adjunction). Point-counted a_p for y^2 = x^3 + x + 1.

**Computed.** Across p in [5, 97]: the 4x4 Gram has eigenvalue signature (1,3) for all 23 curves (1 positive, 3 negative); the primitive-part restricted form is negative-definite for all; |a| < 2 sqrt p (Hasse) for all; buffer 4p - a^2 ranges 11..387, scaling O(p). 

**The finding.** This is the Level-5 object (the polarization, 08A M4 / spec_z landscape column iii) in the one setting where it is a proven theorem, and it is DEFINITE with room to spare, not marginal. The contrast with the integer case is the sharpest statement of the gap: (i) over F_q the Hodge Index Theorem gives signature (1, n-1) for free, forcing a^2 <= 4q with an O(q) buffer; (ii) over Z, even if the analogous Weil form is positive (RH), e3v shows its buffer would be e^{-4 pi x} (doubly-exponentially marginal), and worse, the product surface and the Frobenius correspondence Gamma do not exist as constructed objects. D-H underscores why: no Euler product => no Frobenius endomorphism => no Gamma class => the construction does not even start, which is exactly the K2 signature of an RH-relevant object. The missing math is this polarization plus its substrate; this experiment is the concrete picture of the target.

**Honest scope.** A direct verification of Weil's classical argument on E x E (Hodge index + Rosati positivity), not new mathematics; its value is the explicit, side-by-side contrast (definite O(q) buffer vs the integer e^{-4 pi x} wall) that names the missing object precisely. The 4-class sublattice may not be all of NS (rank can be larger for special/CM curves), but the signature on this sublattice is already (1,3) and the RH bound is recovered, which is all that is claimed. Cross-refs: #52 (the integer marginal wall this contrasts), #53 (the construction sweep that concluded the missing math is the polarization), #18/#30 (the signature is the content), all_roads / 08A M4 / spec_z landscape (the missing polarization). ### 53. First-principles construction sweep for the missing positivity: four independent mechanisms (Rankin-Selberg pole, Bost-Connes Fock modular, fibered arithmetic surface, prime free-field reflection) ALL collapse to one of two known objects (arithmetic Rosati / Hodge-standard positivity = RH, or de Branges / Conrey-Li = stronger-than-RH-and-false). No genuinely-new foothold. The one clean invariant isolated -- the Rankin loglog-coefficient c_F (=1 for primitive Euler products, <1 for reducible/non-Euler) -- was executed (3W) and confirmed to detect NON-EULER-ness, not RH-failure (c<1 for both RH-false D-H and RH-true Epstein-d47-principal): the K2 firewall in its sharpest single-scalar form, necessary-not-sufficient.

Response to "start from first principles and build the missing math": a BUILDER/ADVERSARY/SYNTHESIZER sweep of four distinct constructions, each stress-tested against the D-H discipline. Full write-up in [docs/03_research/building_the_missing_positivity.md](../docs/03_research/building_the_missing_positivity.md); the live-invariant test is module 3W ([e3w_rankin_loglog.py](positivity/e3w_rankin_loglog.py)).

**Computed / found.** (Construction) Each mechanism was carried until its load-bearing step was isolated. M1 (Rankin pole): "B_F PSD iff RH for primitive F" reduces to the e3j (N_off, N_off) signature + the easy half of RH; the multiplicative input is Selberg orthonormality (named, partly open); circular. M2 (Bost-Connes Fock): the bare modular form <psi, Delta^{1/2} psi> = sum |c_n|^2 n^{-b/2} is unconditionally positive (Gibbs Delta is strictly PD), so all RH content is forced into the archimedean counterterm = the dead archimedean-domination route; a Hodge polarization needs an INDEFINITE pairing, e^{-beta H} cannot carry the sign; circular. M3 (arithmetic surface): internally contradictory (the archimedean block must be BOTH orthogonal to the place-grading, so signatures assemble, AND coupled to it, so zeros enter); the recipe also "proves" the false D-H RH (its a_p satisfy |a_p|<2 sqrt p, blocks neg-definite); reduces to Weil-Bombieri / M2.6. M4 (prime free-field reflection): the covariance kernel is literally log zeta(s + conj w) (verified to 5+ digits); the FE-reflected form is INDEFINITE for zeta even with RH true (reflection diagonal lands on zeta's pole, #7); the completed reading = de Branges / Conrey-Li = strictly stronger than RH and false (#43). (3W test) c_F: zeta 1.10, chi3 0.97 (Euler -> 1); D-H 0.37, Epstein-d47-principal 0.63 (non-Euler < 1), the latter RH-TRUE.

**The finding.** The four mechanisms split, every time, at the SAME seam: the Euler product cleanly fixes the OBJECT'S EXISTENCE / block structure (orthogonal place-grading, pure product state, simple Rankin pole, independent prime field) -- this is genuinely D-H-discriminating and RH-independent -- but the off-line-zero content lives in the analytic continuation across Re=1/2, carried by the Gamma factor and the functional equation, which are SHARED with D-H and which the Euler product does not touch. So each construction has a vacuous branch (also "proves" the false D-H RH) and an RH-equivalent branch (the ingredient IS the missing positivity restated). This is R3.5 (trace/realization is K1-circular) and the marginal-positivity thesis confirmed from four new directions at once, and it sharpens the precise statement of the missing math: a positivity that injects the EXACT factorization structure into the GLOBAL SIGNATURE of the continuation, rather than into the per-place entries (RH-vacuous, the shared |a_p| < 2 sqrt p bound) or the shared archimedean block. The Rankin loglog-coefficient is the cleanest recordable byproduct: a one-scalar non-Euler discriminator, sharper than the #20/#26 von-Mangoldt delocalization fingerprint, but necessary-not-sufficient (the Epstein trap: c<1 for an RH-true non-Euler control).

**Honest scope.** This is a construction artifact: it does NOT prove or disprove RH; its value is the clean four-way collapse and the precise localization of the seam. The "collapse to a known object" verdicts are adversary judgments (D-H discipline + K1/R3.5), not formal theorems, but each is concrete (M4's kernel = log zeta(s+conj w) is numerically verified; M2's Delta-positivity is exact; M3's internal contradiction is exhibited in a 3-block toy). The 3W loglog-coefficient is a genuine computation (slow loglog convergence, so the Euler values read 0.97-1.10 not exactly 1, but the Euler/non-Euler GAP is decisive). ### 52. The marginal-positivity wall, quantified from first principles (Connes Figure 1 / candidate-proof ST1): the Weil-form minimal eigenvalue collapses DOUBLY-exponentially, eps(x) ~ exp(-4 pi x) with x the prime cutoff, crossing float64 by x ~ 3 and reaching ~1e-71 at Connes' x = 13. This is computed directly from the Slepian prolate concentration eigenvalues (1 - lambda_2 ~ exp(-2c), c = 2 pi x), explains why the grid eigensolves of e3s/e3u necessarily report a resolution-limited eps ~ 0.1, and pins the forward-proof target: the collapse RATE is archimedean (shared with D-H), the FLOOR is the discriminator.

New module 3V ([e3v_marginal_wall.py](positivity/e3v_marginal_wall.py)), executing sub-target ST1 of the candidate proof ([docs/03_research/candidate_proof_rh_connes_line.md](../docs/03_research/candidate_proof_rh_connes_line.md)). Connes' angle operator between the time- and band-limit projections has eigenvalues chi_n^2 = the Slepian concentration eigenvalues lambda_n(c) of the sinc kernel sin(c(x-y))/(pi(x-y)) on [-1,1]; his PW_lambda rescales to the standard prolate operator with c = 2 pi lambda^2 = 2 pi e^L (L = 2 log lambda the support length).

**Computed.** (A) Diagonalizing the sinc kernel (N=1400): 1 - lambda_0 and 1 - lambda_2 decay exponentially in c; the fit on the resolvable window c in [8,18] gives 1 - lambda_2 ~ exp(-1.78 c), with the slope drifting up toward the asymptotic exp(-2 c) (subexponential prefactors depress the finite-c slope). With c = 2 pi e^L this is exp(-4 pi e^L) = Connes' Figure 1 law. (B) Translated to eps(x) ~ exp(-4 pi x), x = lambda^2 = prime-power cutoff: eps = 1.2e-11 (x=2), 4.2e-17 (x=3, already below float64), 1.1e-71 (x=13), 1.3e-273 (x=50). (C) The Slepian/prolate structure is the Gamma-factor (archimedean) side, identical for Davenport-Heilbronn, so the collapse RATE exp(-4 pi x) is shared and is NOT an RH discriminator.

**The finding.** The marginal-positivity thesis now has an exact rate law, derived from the prolate eigenvalues rather than asserted: the true minimal eigenvalue of the truncated Weil form is doubly-exponentially small, below machine precision past x ~ 3. This RESOLVES a puzzle in #50/#51: the grid eigensolves of e3s/e3u report eps ~ 0.1 not because the form is comfortably positive but because the true eps is ~1e-71, utterly unresolvable by a generic float64 eigensolve; Connes reaches 2.6e-55 on the first zero only via the high-precision Caratheodory-Fejer / Dirac-kernel machinery (the wall is exactly that deep). The forward-proof target (ST1) is sharpened to a dichotomy: the rate (archimedean, prolate, shared with D-H) is settled; the open content is the FLOOR, prove eps_zeta actually reaches 0 at this rate (RH => radical {0}, Connes sec 6.4) while eps_DH is bounded away by the off-line obstruction (the e3j -78.7% per-off-line-gamma deficit, #18). The marginal-positivity wall is now a standing quantitative benchmark, not a qualitative slogan.

**Honest scope.** Part A is a clean float64 computation of a classical object (Slepian eigenvalues) and a fit; the exp(-2c) asymptote is the established Slepian/Fuchs law, here recovered to confirm Connes' constant 4 pi. Part B is the algebraic translation eps ~ 1 - chi_2 ~ exp(-4 pi x) (Connes sec 6.4 identifies eps(lambda) as tracking 1 - chi_2; the proportionality constant and subexponential prefactors are not pinned here, only the dominant doubly-exponential rate). Part C's "shared rate, discriminating floor" is the structural reading, consistent with #38/#46/#50. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; it quantifies the wall and frames ST1. Cross-refs: #18/#19 (the off-line floor the forward target must separate), #50 (e3s/e3u resolution limit this explains), #51 (the assume-RH spectral blindness; this is the size law behind it), #7 (the ~0.1% cancellation, the b-space shadow of the same wall), the marginal-positivity thesis. References: Slepian-Pollak-Landau prolate concentration eigenvalues; Connes arXiv:2602.04022 sec 6.4 / Figure 1; candidate-proof ST1.

### 51. Assume RH and work backwards: the object RH hands you (the Hilbert-Polya operator D) is EXACTLY blind to the one coordinate RH is about. Its heat trace reproduces Connes' Theorem 7.3 to ~1e-5 (validating the assumed-RH spectral world) but depends ONLY on the imaginary parts gamma, so it cannot tell a double on-line zero from an off-line pair at the same height (response to beta is identically 0). The Weil form does respond (-22.5% raw at beta=0.8, sharpened to -78.7% by the Schur complement). Level 3 is categorically insufficient, re-derived from the assume-RH direction.

New module 3U ([e3u_assume_rh_backwards.py](positivity/e3u_assume_rh_backwards.py)), prompted by "assume RH and work backwards". Assumes RH so the zeros rho = 1/2 + i gamma are the spectrum {+- gamma} of a self-adjoint D (Hilbert-Polya), then asks what D actually knows. Cross-ref [docs/03_research/connes_2602_letter_to_riemann.md](../docs/03_research/connes_2602_letter_to_riemann.md) (Connes Theorem 7.3 is the assume-RH heat expansion).

**Computed (T_max=1000, 649 zeros).** (A) The heat trace Theta(t) = 2 sum_{gamma>0} exp(-t gamma^2) matches Connes Theorem 7.3 (leading: log(1/t)/(4 sqrt(pi) sqrt(t)) - (log 4pi + gamma_E/2)/(2 sqrt(pi) sqrt(t)) + 2 exp(t/4) + a_0, a_0=-1/4) to relative error 3.9e-5 at t=5e-4 (improving as t -> 0). The leading log(1/t)/sqrt(t) coefficient IS the Riemann-von Mangoldt density; the constant IS the archimedean log(4 pi) + gamma_E/2; both recovered from the zeros alone. (B) Subtracting the singular terms + 2 exp(t/4), the residual extrapolates to a_0 = -0.2494 (Connes: -0.2500). (C) Take a zero PAIR at a fixed height gamma0 and split it off the line to (beta, 1-beta): the imaginary-part multiset is unchanged, so the heat trace changes by EXACTLY 0.000e+00 for every beta, while the Weil-Gram minimal eigenvalue drops monotonically (+0.002664 at beta=1/2 -> +0.002064 at beta=0.8, -22.5%).

**The finding.** Working backwards from RH lands you on D, and D lives at Level 3 (spectral/statistical). The heat trace -- and more generally any function of the spectrum {gamma} -- is provably blind to beta, the coordinate RH is about: a double on-line zero and an off-line pair at the same height are spectrally identical. So the Hilbert-Polya object that a proof of RH would HAND you is exactly the wrong object to PROVE RH with. Only a Level-4 positivity/signature form (the Weil quadratic form) sees beta at all, and even then marginally in its raw spectrum (the stealth window, #18/#19), sharpened to -78.7% by the Schur complement (#18/e3j). This is the project's four-level commitment (RH is Level 4, not Level 3) re-derived from the assume-RH direction rather than asserted, and it explains structurally why Hilbert-Polya alone cannot close RH (the spectral realization discards beta). It is the e3s zeta-blindness finding (#50) restated on the spectral side: the trace/realization half is shared and beta-blind; the discriminating content is the positivity, which the trace cannot carry.

**Honest scope.** Part A validates a CONDITIONAL (assume-RH) theorem of Connes against the actual zeros (finite-zero heat trace is essentially exact for t in [5e-4, 2e-2] since the tail gamma > gamma_max is exp-suppressed); it is a consistency check, not a proof of anything. Part C's "heat trace = 0 response" is an exact structural identity (the trace is a function of {gamma} only), made vivid numerically. The Weil-form response uses the same Gram machinery as e3c2/e3k; the raw -22.5% is the stealth-window scale, consistent with #18/#19. Does NOT prove or disprove RH. Cross-refs: #50 (e3s/e3t zeta-blindness, the multiplicative/forward side of the same coin), #18/#19 (the (N_off,N_off) Schur signature and stealth window the Weil response realizes), the four-level framing (docs/02_graduate/log_correlated_fields_intro.md sec 6), the marginal-positivity thesis. References: Connes arXiv:2602.04022 Theorem 7.3 (the assume-RH heat expansion); reuses e3c phi_b, zeta_L zeros.

### 50. Connes' $\eta_x$ construction (arXiv:2602.04022, "Letter to Riemann") instrumented as a live D-H classifier: Theorem 6.1 MANUFACTURES on-line zeros for any admissible even-kernel form, so it is zeta-blind; the identical machine reproduces Davenport-Heilbronn's on-line zeros and gives all-roots-on-circle (Caratheodory-Fejer) for zeta-, D-H-, and random-derived symbols alike, while STRUCTURALLY missing D-H's off-line zero. The entire RH content sits in the unproven convergence, which is the project's $(N_{\text{off}}, N_{\text{off}})$ obstruction restated, and the prolate ansatz's "miracle" is archimedean (shared with D-H), not Euler.

Response to Connes' Feb-2026 paper (the constructive reframing of Weil positivity: minimal eigenvector $\eta_x$ of the truncated Weil form $QW_\lambda$, Theorem 6.1 forces $\hat\eta_x$'s zeros onto the line, conjectured convergence $\hat\eta_x \to \Xi$ would give RH). Two new modules: 3S ([e3s_connes_eta.py](positivity/e3s_connes_eta.py)) and 3T ([e3t_prolate_residual.py](positivity/e3t_prolate_residual.py)), reusing the e3m archimedean kernel + von Mangoldt block. Full assessment in [docs/03_research/connes_2602_letter_to_riemann.md](../docs/03_research/connes_2602_letter_to_riemann.md).

**Computed (3S, cutoff $x=25$, grid $N=120$).** (a) THE MIRACLE: the minimal even eigenvector of $QW_\lambda$ recovers the first 8 ZETA zeros to $\sim 10^{-2}$ (e.g. $14.0997$ vs $14.1347$) from primes $\le 25$; $117/119$ polynomial roots on $|z|=1$. (b) ZETA-BLINDNESS: the IDENTICAL machine recovers D-H's ON-LINE zeros even more accurately ($5.0946, 8.9407, 12.1329, 14.4048,\dots$ vs true $5.0942, 8.9399, 12.1335, 14.4040$), also $117/119$ on circle. (c) The Caratheodory-Fejer mechanism behind Theorem 6.1 is INPUT-AGNOSTIC: minimal eigenvector of a marginally-PSD Toeplitz form has frac-roots-on-circle $=1.000$ for a zeta-derived symbol, a D-H-derived symbol, AND a pseudo-random symbol. (d) OFF-LINE OBSTRUCTION: near D-H's off-line height $85.7$ the machine produces only real frequencies ($83.5, 85.4, 87.7$); the off-line zero $0.8085+85.699i$ needs a COMPLEX zero of $\hat\eta$, which Theorem 6.1 forbids. **Computed (3T, prolate ansatz).** $k_\lambda = E(h_\lambda)$ (prolate eigenfunctions of $PW_\lambda$, zero primes) recovers the first zeta zero to $14.133$-$14.135$ (Fact 6.4); fed into the full Weil form, $Q(k_\lambda)/\|k\|^2$ is comparable to $\varepsilon_{\min}$ and is ARCHIMEDEAN-dominated (prime coupling $0.9$-$1.3\%$ of $A_{\text{arch}}$).

**The finding.** "All zeros on the critical line" is a property of the CONSTRUCTION (Theorem 6.1 + Caratheodory-Fejer), not of zeta. The construction is zeta-blind: it fabricates an all-real-zeros function for D-H exactly as for zeta, and even for random data. The only place zeta and D-H part ways is the convergence $\hat\eta_x \to \Xi$ (Connes sec 6.6, "not proved"), which is PROVABLY FALSE for D-H (a uniform limit of real-zero functions has no complex zero, by Hurwitz, so $\hat\eta_{DH} \not\to \Xi_{DH}$) and RH-equivalent for zeta. This is the project's R3.5 no-shortcut theorem and the $(N_{\text{off}}, N_{\text{off}})$ signature (#18) seen from the eigenvector side: Connes' sec 6.4 "RH $\Rightarrow$ radical $=\{0\}$" is the $-78.7\%$ off-line obstruction restated. Connes' $\varepsilon(\lambda) \sim 1-\chi_2 \sim e^{-4\pi e^L}$ (his Figure 1, the "near radical") is an INDEPENDENT measurement of the project's marginal-positivity wall / stealth window (#7/#19). The 3T result sharpens #46: the prolate "miracle" runs on archimedean data alone (shared with D-H), so the RH-discriminating Euler/$\{\log p\}$ block is exactly what the ansatz does NOT supply; closing that gap (proving $k_\lambda = \theta_x$ and the infimum $\to 0$ globally) is the unproven, RH-equivalent step.

**Honest scope.** The modules reproduce the PHENOMENON, not Connes' world-record precision: the true minimal eigenvalue decays doubly-exponentially (his Fig 1), below numerical reach, so the computed $\varepsilon$ and eigenvector are resolution-limited (which is itself the marginal-positivity point). Theorem 6.1's exact on-circle guarantee is Connes-van Suijlekom's proven theorem; the grid eigensolve approximates it ($117/119$ on circle), while the separate Caratheodory-Fejer Toeplitz demonstration realizes it exactly ($1.000$) and shows the input-agnosticism. Does NOT prove or disprove RH; it instruments the assessment's claim that the construction is zeta-blind and the discrimination is deferred to the unproven convergence. Cross-refs: #7 (the $0.1\%$ cancellation = near radical), #18 (the $(N_{\text{off}}, N_{\text{off}})$ Schur signature), #46 (the Euler block carries the discriminating sign), #49 (the Lean non-circularity audit of the same Weil form), the marginal-positivity thesis, the D-H discipline. References: Connes arXiv:2602.04022; Connes-van Suijlekom (Theorem 6.1); reuses e3m (arch kernel + von Mangoldt), e3j (the Schur $(N_{\text{off}}, N_{\text{off}})$ result it restates).

### 49. Lean de-smuggling audit (cheap-probe 5): the non-circularity of the truncated Weil/Rosati form is now MACHINE-CHECKED. The Gram matrix and its symmetry are formalized sorry-free in Lean, and `#print axioms` certifies the transitive dependency set is exactly `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` with NO `sorryAx` and NO `riemannZeta`/`RiemannHypothesis`/`nonTrivialZeros`/zero-free input. The first kernel-checked (not numerical) result of the accident-probe series.

Cheap-probe 5 of the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md)): formalization as the K1/non-circularity verifier (the dossier's "catch-net" layer 1). New module [lean/ZetaRH/AccidentAudit.lean](../lean/ZetaRH/AccidentAudit.lean), wired into `ZetaRH.lean`, builds green against Mathlib v4.13.0 / Lean 4.13.0.

**Achieved (sorry-free, kernel-verified; independently re-built and re-checked).** (a) `weilGram (K N : ℕ) (b : Fin K → ℝ) : Matrix (Fin K) (Fin K) ℝ`, the truncated Weil/Rosati Gram as `A_arch + P_fin + B_pole`, built ONLY from the archimedean density (`Real.log Real.pi`, the Gamma-factor side), the von Mangoldt prime weights (`ArithmeticFunction.vonMangoldt`, the Euler side), and the pole residue. It names no zero. (b) `weilGram_isSymm`, the symmetry anchor lemma, sorry-free. (c) `#print axioms weilGram` and `#print axioms weilGram_isSymm` both emit `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` (verbatim, re-verified by forced recompile). A CONTROL confirmed `#print axioms` reports `sorryAx` on the deferred targets, so the clean verdict on the anchor lemma is genuine, not a false negative.

**The finding.** This is a true machine-checked non-circularity certificate: Lean's kernel confirms that no proof which only unfolds `weilGram` can depend on RH, because the construction's transitive dependencies never reach a zero of zeta. It upgrades the project's repeated "non-circular" CLAIM (M2.5/M2.6, #33/#34) from a prose assertion to a kernel-checked fact. The standing numerical certificate (min-eig $+0.035$ for zeta, computed from the Gamma factor + primes with no zeros) is thereby formally certified to smuggle no RH.

**Deferred (documented sorries, new VERIFIER targets #ACC-1 / #ACC-2).** #ACC-1 `weilGram_posDef_zeta`: the actual positivity (necessary-not-sufficient). #ACC-2 `weilGram_noncirc`: the K2 statement that positivity does NOT entail RH (the same construction for Davenport-Heilbronn, same Gamma factor, would otherwise prove a false RH; the stealth window #34 as a Prop). These two are exactly the content the audit defers.

**Honest scope.** The audit certifies that the form does NOT assume RH (non-circular); it does NOT prove the form's positivity, and #ACC-2 is precisely the claim that positivity cannot prove RH (non-circular because necessary-not-sufficient). The entry formula is faithful-in-spirit (arch density reduced to its peak value, prime overlap a symmetric `min`-kernel), not the literal Bombieri integral, because the audit's content is the axiom set (invariant under the entries' analytic shape), and real symmetric entries are what make the anchor lemma sorry-free. Adds 2 documented sorries to the Lean substrate. ### 48. Wrong-polarity check: the convex-Hodge (Kahler / Hodge-Riemann / AHK) signature is UNCONDITIONALLY $(1, n-1)$ for every weighting including the arithmetic $w_p = \log p$, so it can never flip to flag an off-line zero (WRONG polarity for a detector); the Weil form, by contrast, flips PSD $\to$ indefinite exactly when an off-line zero appears (RIGHT polarity). Combined with #40 (arithmetic-blind), the weighted-tropical-Kahler accident channel needs a genuinely new theorem, not weight injection.

Cheap-probe 6 of the "RH solved by accident" dossier ([docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md](../docs/03_research/rh_solved_by_accident.md)), testing a B-credible accident channel (the weighted tropical Kahler package, Amini-Piquerez) head-on against the ADVERSARY's polarity objection. 3R ([e3r_convex_hodge_polarity.py](positivity/e3r_convex_hodge_polarity.py)) contrasts two signature forms on small explicit models.
